목차 · 시험 행동 그래프 · 요구별 API 호출 · 전체 API 검색

전체 소스 위치 보기

현재 작성 자료가 참조하는 정규 원본이다. SDK와 프로젝트별 framework 중복 사본은 제외했다. 각 줄의 링크는 HTML 안의 실제 원문 줄을 가리킨다.

171 } 172  173 inline int Application::run(const ApplicationConfig& config) { 174     Diagnostics::configure(config.diagnostics); 175     Diagnostics::clear(); 176     if (running_) { 177         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Runtime,"Application::run","run() 재귀 호출은 허용하지 않습니다."); 187     try { 188         if (!initialize(config)) { 189             std::cerr << "애플리케이션 초기화 실패.\n"; 190             exitCode = -1; 191         } else if (initHandler_ && !initHandler_(*this)) { 192             Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"Application::initHandler","사용자 초기화 콜백이 false를 반환했습니다.","앞선 API 오류와 장면 생성 콜백을 확인하세요."); 193             std::cerr << "사용자 장면 초기화 실패.\n"; 194             exitCode = -1; 195         } else { 196             double previousTime = detail::glfw().GetTime(); 197             while (!detail::glfw().WindowShouldClose(window_)) { 198                 Diagnostics::locate(__FILE__,__LINE__,"run -> glfwPollEvents/input callbacks"); 199                 detail::glfw().PollEvents(); 200                 if(callbackError_)std::rethrow_exception(callbackError_); 201                 if (detail::glfw().WindowShouldClose(window_)) { 202                     break; 203                 } 204                 int width = 0; 205                 int height = 0; 206                 detail::glfw().GetFramebufferSize(window_, &width, &height); 207                 if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) { 208                     detail::glfw().WaitEvents(); 209                     if(callbackError_)std::rethrow_exception(callbackError_); 210                     previousTime = detail::glfw().GetTime(); 211                     continue; 212                 } 213                 const double currentTime = detail::glfw().GetTime(); 214                 const float deltaTime = static_cast<float>( 215                     std::clamp(currentTime - previousTime, 0.0, 0.05)); 216                 previousTime = currentTime; 217                 if (updateHandler_) { 218                     Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Input, "Application::updateHandler"); 219                     updateHandler_(*this, deltaTime); 220                 } 221                 if (detail::glfw().WindowShouldClose(window_)) { 222                     break; 223                 } 224                 if (!scene_.updateMotions(deltaTime)) { 225                     std::cerr << "모션 갱신 실패.\n"; 226                     exitCode = -1; 227                     break; 228                 } 229                 if(!ensureContext("run -> render callback",__FILE__,__LINE__)) {exitCode=-1;break;} 230                 const bool rendered = renderHandler_ 231                     ? renderHandler_(*this) : renderScene(); 232                 if (!rendered) { 233                     std::cerr << "렌더링 실패.\n"; 234                     exitCode = -1; 235                     break; 236                 } 237                 Diagnostics::locate(__FILE__,__LINE__,"run -> glfwSwapBuffers"); 238                 const auto swapSerial=detail::contextErrorSerial(); 239                 detail::glfw().SwapBuffers(window_); 240                 if(swapSerial!=detail::contextErrorSerial()) {contextFailed_=true;exitCode=-1;break;} 241             } 242         } 243     } catch (const std::exception& error) { 244         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::run", error.what(), "앞선 파이프라인 단계/API 오류를 확인하세요. 사용자 콜백 예외는 자원 정리 후 다시 전달됩니다."); 245         shutdown(); 246         running_ = false; 247         detail::applicationActive()=false; 248         throw; 249     } catch (...) { 250         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::run", "콜백 또는 실행 처리에서 표준 예외가 아닌 예외가 발생했습니다."); 251         shutdown(); 252         running_ = false; 253         detail::applicationActive()=false; 254         throw; // 사용자 예외를 숨기지 않고 컨텍스트 자원부터 정리한다. 255     } 256     shutdown(); 257     running_ = false; 258     detail::applicationActive()=false; 259     return exitCode; 260 } 261  262 inline void Application::setMouseHandler(std::function<void(Application&,const MouseEvent&)> handler) { 263     mouseHandler_=std::move(handler);    1 #pragma once   2 #include <algorithm>   3 #include <stdexcept>   4    5 namespace graphics {   6 inline bool Motion::validStep(const MotionStep& step) {   7     return bool(step.transform) && std::isfinite(step.startSeconds) && step.startSeconds >= 0   8         && (!step.durationSeconds || (std::isfinite(*step.durationSeconds)   9             && *step.durationSeconds >= 0  10             && std::isfinite(step.startSeconds + *step.durationSeconds)));  11 }  12 inline bool Motion::append(const MotionStep& step) { return insert(steps_.size(), step); }  13 inline bool Motion::insert(std::size_t index, const MotionStep& step) {  14     if (index > steps_.size() || !validStep(step)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::insert", "Rejected condition: index > steps_.size() || !validStep(step)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");  15     steps_.insert(steps_.begin() + index, step);  16     return true;  17 }  18 inline bool Motion::replace(std::size_t index, const MotionStep& step) {  19     if (index >= steps_.size() || !validStep(step)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::replace", "Rejected condition: index >= steps_.size() || !validStep(step)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");  20     steps_[index] = step;  21     return true;  22 }  23 inline bool Motion::erase(std::size_t index) {  24     if (index >= steps_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::erase", "Rejected condition: index >= steps_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");  25     steps_.erase(steps_.begin() + index);  26     return true;  27 }  28 inline bool Motion::then(MotionStep step) {  29     double end = 0;  30     for (const auto& current : steps_) {  31         if (!current.durationSeconds) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::then", "Rejected condition: !current.durationSeconds", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");  32         end = std::max(end, current.startSeconds + *current.durationSeconds);  33     }  34     step.startSeconds += end;  35     return append(step);  36 }  37 inline std::optional<MotionSample> Motion::sample(double seconds) const {  38     if (!std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample", "Rejected condition: !std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);  39     MotionSample result;  40     for (const auto& step : steps_) {  41         if (!step.enabled || seconds < step.startSeconds) continue;  42         double time = seconds - step.startSeconds;  43         if (step.durationSeconds) time = std::min(time, *step.durationSeconds);  44         const auto matrix = step.transform(time);  45         if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample", "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);  46         auto& target = step.space == TransformSpace::World ? result.parent : result.local;  47         target = matrix * target; // Within each space, API order is application order.  48         if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(target)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample", "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(target)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);  49     }  50     return result;  51 }  52 inline MotionStep Motion::fixed(const glm::mat4& matrix, TransformSpace space) {  53     MotionStep step;  54     if (TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix)) step.transform = [matrix](double) { return matrix; };  55     step.space = space;  56     step.durationSeconds = 0;  57     return step;  58 }  59 inline MotionStep Motion::rotation(const RotationMotion& config) {  60     MotionStep step;  61     if (!std::isfinite(config.degreesPerSecond) || !std::isfinite(config.initialDegrees)  62         || !TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis, config.initialDegrees)) return step;  63     step.space = config.space;  64     step.transform = [config](double seconds) {  65         const auto matrix = TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis,  66             config.initialDegrees + config.degreesPerSecond * seconds);  67         if (!matrix) throw std::domain_error("Rotation motion produced an invalid matrix.");  68         return *matrix;  69     };  70     return step;  71 }  72 inline MotionStep Motion::followPath(const PathMotion& config) {  73     MotionStep step;  74     if (!config.path || !config.interpolation || !std::isfinite(config.durationSeconds)  75         || config.durationSeconds <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.rate) || config.rate == 0  76         || !std::isfinite(config.phase) || config.phase < 0 || config.phase > 1) return step;  77     step.space = config.space;  78     if (config.playback == Playback::Once) {  79         step.durationSeconds = (config.rate > 0 ? 1 - config.phase : config.phase)  80                              * config.durationSeconds / std::abs(config.rate);  81         if (!std::isfinite(*step.durationSeconds)) return MotionStep{};  82     }  83     step.transform = [config](double seconds) {  84         double progress = config.phase + seconds / config.durationSeconds * config.rate;  85         if (!std::isfinite(progress)) throw std::domain_error("Path time overflow.");  86         if (config.playback == Playback::Once) progress = std::clamp(progress, 0.0, 1.0);  87         else if (config.playback == Playback::Loop) progress -= std::floor(progress);  88         else {  89             progress -= std::floor(progress / 2) * 2;  90             if (progress > 1) progress = 2 - progress;  91         }  92         const double u = config.interpolation(progress);  93         if (!std::isfinite(u)) throw std::domain_error("Interpolation returned non-finite progress.");  94         glm::vec3 offset = config.path(u);  95         if (config.relativeToStart) {  96             const double initial = config.interpolation(config.phase);  97             if (!std::isfinite(initial)) throw std::domain_error("Invalid initial interpolation.");  98             offset -= config.path(initial);  99         } 100         const auto matrix = TransformSystem::translation(offset); 101         if (!matrix) throw std::domain_error("Path returned a non-finite position."); 102         return *matrix; 103     }; 104     return step; 105 } 106 inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createMotion(const Motion& motion) { 107     const auto sample = motion.sample(0); 108     if (!sample || nextMotion_ == std::numeric_limits<MotionId>::max()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample || nextMotion_ == std::numeric_limits<MotionId>::max()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 109     const auto id = nextMotion_; 110     MotionState state; 111     state.motion = motion; 112     state.sampled = *sample; 113     motions_.emplace(id, std::move(state)); 114     ++nextMotion_; 115     return id; 116 } 117 inline const MotionState* MotionSystem::getMotion(MotionId id) const { 118     const auto found = motions_.find(id); 119     return found == motions_.end() ? nullptr : &found->second; 120 } 121 inline bool MotionSystem::setMotion(MotionId id, const Motion& motion) { 122     const auto found = motions_.find(id); 124     const auto sample = motion.sample(found->second.timeSeconds); 125     if (!sample) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 126     found->second.motion = motion; 127     found->second.sampled = *sample; 128     return true; 129 } 130 inline bool MotionSystem::setMotionPlaying(MotionId id, bool playing) { 131     const auto found = motions_.find(id); 139     found->second.enabled = enabled; 140     return true; 141 } 142 inline bool MotionSystem::seekMotion(MotionId id, double seconds) { 143     const auto found = motions_.find(id); 144     if (found == motions_.end()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::seekMotion", "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 145     const auto sample = found->second.motion.sample(seconds); 146     if (!sample) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::seekMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 147     found->second.timeSeconds = seconds; 148     found->second.sampled = *sample; 149     return true; 150 } 151 inline bool MotionSystem::removeMotion(MotionId id) { 152     if (!motions_.erase(id)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::removeMotion", "Rejected condition: !motions_.erase(id)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 153     for (auto& layer : layers_) 154         if (layer.second.config.motion == id) layer.second.config.motion.reset(); 155     return true; 156 } 157 inline bool MotionSystem::update(double deltaSeconds) { 158     if (!std::isfinite(deltaSeconds) || deltaSeconds < 0) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::update", "Rejected condition: !std::isfinite(deltaSeconds) || deltaSeconds < 0", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."); 159     struct Pending { MotionId id; double time; MotionSample sample; }; 160     std::vector<Pending> pending; 161     pending.reserve(motions_.size()); 162     for (const auto& entry : motions_) { 163         const auto& state = entry.second; 164         if (!state.playing) continue; 165         const double time = state.timeSeconds + deltaSeconds; 166         const auto sample = state.motion.sample(time); 167         if (!sample) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::update", "Rejected condition: !sample", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."); 168         pending.push_back({entry.first, time, *sample}); 169     } 170     for (const auto& item : pending) { 171         auto& state = motions_.at(item.id); 172         state.timeSeconds = item.time; 173         state.sampled = item.sample; 174     } 175     return true; 176 } 177 inline void MotionSystem::clear() { 178     std::map<LayerId, LayerState> emptyLayers{{rootLayer(), LayerState{}}}; 179     motions_.clear(); 180     layers_.swap(emptyLayers); 181 } 182 inline bool MotionSystem::validLayer(const LayerConfig& config) const { 183     return TransformSystem::validAffine(config.transform) 184         && (!config.motion || getMotion(*config.motion)); 185 } 186 inline const LayerState* MotionSystem::getLayer(LayerId id) const { 187     const auto found = layers_.find(id); 188     return found == layers_.end() ? nullptr : &found->second; 189 } 190 inline std::optional<LayerId> MotionSystem::pushLayer(LayerId parent, const LayerConfig& config) { 191     if (!getLayer(parent) || !validLayer(config) 192         || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::pushLayer", "Rejected condition: !getLayer(parent) || !validLayer(config) || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 193     auto staged = layers_; 194     const auto id = nextLayer_; 195     staged.emplace(id, LayerState{config, parent, {}}); 196     staged.at(parent).children.push_back(id); 197     layers_.swap(staged); 198     ++nextLayer_; 199     return id; 200 } 201 inline std::optional<LayerId> MotionSystem::insertLayer(LayerId child, const LayerConfig& config) { 202     const auto current = getLayer(child); 203     if (!current || !current->parent || !validLayer(config) 204         || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::insertLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent || !validLayer(config) || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 205     const auto parent = *current->parent; 206     const auto id = nextLayer_; 207     auto staged = layers_; 208     staged.emplace(id, LayerState{config, parent, {child}}); 209     auto& siblings = staged.at(parent).children; 210     std::replace(siblings.begin(), siblings.end(), child, id); 211     staged.at(child).parent = id; 212     layers_.swap(staged); 213     ++nextLayer_; 214     return id; 215 } 216 inline bool MotionSystem::removeLayer(LayerId id) { 217     const auto current = getLayer(id); 218     if (!current || !current->parent) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::removeLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 219     auto staged = layers_; 220     const auto parent = *current->parent; 221     auto& siblings = staged.at(parent).children; 222     const auto position = std::find(siblings.begin(), siblings.end(), id); 223     const auto index = std::size_t(position - siblings.begin()); 224     siblings.erase(position); 225     siblings.insert(siblings.begin() + index, current->children.begin(), current->children.end()); 226     for (auto child : current->children) staged.at(child).parent = parent; 227     staged.erase(id); 228     layers_.swap(staged); 229     return true; 230 } 231 inline std::optional<LayerId> MotionSystem::popLayer(LayerId top) { 232     const auto current = getLayer(top); 233     if (!current || !current->parent || !current->children.empty()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::popLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent || !current->children.empty()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 234     const auto parent = *current->parent; 235     if (!removeLayer(top)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::popLayer", "Rejected condition: !removeLayer(top)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 236     return parent; 237 } 238 inline bool MotionSystem::reparentLayer(LayerId id, LayerId parent) { 239     const auto current = getLayer(id); 240     if (!current || !current->parent || !getLayer(parent)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::reparentLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent || !getLayer(parent)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 241     if (current->parent == parent) return true; 242     auto ancestor = std::optional<LayerId>(parent); 243     while (ancestor) { 244         if (*ancestor == id) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::reparentLayer", "Rejected condition: *ancestor == id", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 245         ancestor = layers_.at(*ancestor).parent; 246     } 247     auto staged = layers_; 248     auto& old = staged.at(*current->parent).children; 249     old.erase(std::find(old.begin(), old.end(), id)); 250     staged.at(parent).children.push_back(id); 251     staged.at(id).parent = parent; 252     layers_.swap(staged); 253     return true; 254 } 255 inline bool MotionSystem::setLayer(LayerId id, const LayerConfig& config) { 256     const auto found = layers_.find(id); 257     if (found == layers_.end() || !validLayer(config)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setLayer", "Rejected condition: found == layers_.end() || !validLayer(config)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 258     found->second.config = config; 259     return true; 260 } 261 inline bool MotionSystem::bindMotion(LayerId layer, std::optional<MotionId> motion) { 262     const auto current = getLayer(layer); 263     if (!current || (motion && !getMotion(*motion))) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::bindMotion", "Rejected condition: !current || (motion && !getMotion(*motion))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."); 264     auto config = current->config; 265     config.motion = motion; 266     return setLayer(layer, config); 267 } 268 inline std::optional<MotionSample> MotionSystem::layerMatrices(LayerId leaf) const { 269     std::vector<LayerId> chain; 270     auto current = std::optional<LayerId>(leaf); 271     while (current) { 272         const auto layer = getLayer(*current); 273         if (!layer || chain.size() >= layers_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::layerMatrices", "Rejected condition: !layer || chain.size() >= layers_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 274         chain.push_back(*current); 275         current = layer->parent; 276     } 277     MotionSample result; 278     for (auto i = chain.rbegin(); i != chain.rend(); ++i) { 279         const auto& config = layers_.at(*i).config; 280         if (!config.enabled) continue; 281         MotionSample own; 282         if (config.space == TransformSpace::World) own.parent = config.transform; 283         else own.local = config.transform; 284         if (config.motion) { 285             const auto state = getMotion(*config.motion); 286             if (!state) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::layerMatrices", "Rejected condition: !state", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 287             if (state->enabled) { 288                 own.parent = state->sampled.parent * own.parent; 289                 own.local = state->sampled.local * own.local; 290             } 291         } 292         if (*i == rootLayer()) result.parent = own.parent * own.local; 293         else { 294             result.parent *= own.parent; 295             result.local *= own.local; 296         } 297         if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(result.parent) 298             || !TransformSystem::validAffine(result.local)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::layerMatrices", "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(result.parent) || !TransformSystem::validAffine(result.local)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 299     } 300     return result; 301 } 302 inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createSpin(RotationMotion config) { 303     config.space = TransformSpace::Local; 304     Motion motion; 305     if (!motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createSpin", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 306     return createMotion(motion); 307 } 308 inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createOrbit(RotationMotion config) { 309     config.space = TransformSpace::World; 310     Motion motion; 311     if (!motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createOrbit", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 312     return createMotion(motion); 313 } 314 inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createOrbitAndSpin(RotationMotion orbit, 315                                                             RotationMotion spin) { 316     orbit.space = TransformSpace::World; 317     spin.space = TransformSpace::Local; 318     Motion motion; 319     if (!motion.append(Motion::rotation(orbit)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createOrbitAndSpin", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::rotation(orbit)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 320     return createMotion(motion); 321 } 322 inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createPathAndSpin(const PathMotion& path, 323                                                            RotationMotion spin) { 324     spin.space = TransformSpace::Local; 325     Motion motion; 326     if (!motion.append(Motion::followPath(path)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createPathAndSpin", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::followPath(path)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 327     return createMotion(motion); 328 } 329 } // namespace graphics

return api; } 255 inline bool checkGlAt(const char* file,int line,PipelineStage stage, const char* api) { 256     bool success = true; 257     for (GLenum code = gl().GetError(); code != GL_NO_ERROR; code = gl().GetError()) { 258         const char* cause = code == GL_INVALID_ENUM ? "GL_INVALID_ENUM: unsupported enum" 259             : code == GL_INVALID_VALUE ? "GL_INVALID_VALUE: value outside allowed range" 260             : code == GL_INVALID_OPERATION ? "GL_INVALID_OPERATION: incompatible binding, state or program" 261             : code == GL_OUT_OF_MEMORY ? "GL_OUT_OF_MEMORY: GPU allocation failed" 262             : code == GL_INVALID_FRAMEBUFFER_OPERATION ? "GL_INVALID_FRAMEBUFFER_OPERATION: incomplete framebuffer" 263             : "OpenGL error"; 264         Diagnostics::failAt(file,line,stage, api, std::string(cause) + " (" + std::to_string(code) + ")", 265             "Inspect this stage's bindings, buffer sizes, vertex layout, shader log and current context. A GL error does not identify a single root cause."); 266         success = false; 267     } 268     return success; 269 } 270 } // namespace graphics::detail
framework/detail/ObjLoader.inl
   1 #pragma once   2 #include <fstream>   3 #include <sstream>   4 #include <locale>   5 #include <iterator>   6 #include <iostream>   7    8 namespace graphics::detail {   9 struct ObjParseError : std::runtime_error { using std::runtime_error::runtime_error; };  10 struct ObjCorner {  11     long long position=0, uv=0, normal=0;  12     bool operator==(const ObjCorner& b) const {  13         return position==b.position && uv==b.uv && normal==b.normal;  14     }  15 };  16 struct ObjFace {  17     std::vector<ObjCorner> corners;  18     std::size_t positions=0, uvs=0, normals=0, line=0, batch=0;  19     std::string name;  20 };  21 inline void objWarning(std::vector<std::string>& warnings,const std::string& message) {  22     if(std::find(warnings.begin(),warnings.end(),message)==warnings.end()) warnings.push_back(message);  23 }  24 inline long long objInteger(const std::string& text) {  25     const auto start=(!text.empty() && (text[0]=='-' || text[0]=='+'))?1u:0u;  26     if(start==text.size() || text.find_first_not_of("0123456789",start)!=std::string::npos)  27         throw ObjParseError("Invalid OBJ index: "+text);  28     try {  29         const auto value=std::stoll(text);  30         if(value==0) throw ObjParseError("OBJ indices start at 1; zero is invalid.");  31         return value;  32     } catch(const std::out_of_range&) { throw ObjParseError("OBJ index is too large."); }  33 }  34 inline ObjCorner objCorner(const std::string& text) {  35     ObjCorner result;  36     const auto a=text.find('/'), b=a==std::string::npos?a:text.find('/',a+1);  37     if(b!=std::string::npos && text.find('/',b+1)!=std::string::npos)  38         throw ObjParseError("Too many fields in OBJ corner: "+text);  39     result.position=objInteger(text.substr(0,a));  40     if(a!=std::string::npos) {  41         const auto part=text.substr(a+1,b==std::string::npos?b:b-a-1);  42         if(!part.empty()) result.uv=objInteger(part);  43         else if(b==std::string::npos) throw ObjParseError("Missing UV after '/'.");  44     }  45     if(b!=std::string::npos) {  46         if(b+1==text.size()) throw ObjParseError("Missing normal after '//'.");  47         result.normal=objInteger(text.substr(b+1));  48     }  49     return result;  50 }  51 inline std::size_t objResolve(long long value,std::size_t atFace,std::size_t total,bool compatible) {  52     // Negative references use counts at the face, even during the second pass.  53     if(value<0) {  54         if(value < -static_cast<long long>(atFace)) throw ObjParseError("Negative OBJ index is out of range.");  55         return std::size_t(static_cast<long long>(atFace)+value);  56     }  57     if(static_cast<unsigned long long>(value)>(compatible?total:atFace))  58         throw ObjParseError("Positive OBJ index is out of range or references a later record.");  59     return std::size_t(value-1);  60 }  61 inline std::vector<float> objNumbers(std::istringstream& row) {  62     std::vector<float> result;  63     std::string token;  64     while(row>>token) {  65         std::istringstream number(token); number.imbue(std::locale::classic());  66         double value=0; char extra=0;  67         if(!(number>>value) || (number>>extra) || !std::isfinite(value)  68             || std::abs(value)>std::numeric_limits<float>::max())  69             throw ObjParseError("Invalid finite OBJ number: "+token);  70         result.push_back(float(value));  71     }  72     return result;  73 }  74 inline std::string objText(const std::string& bytes,bool compatible,std::vector<std::string>& warnings) {  75     std::string text=bytes;  76     if(bytes.size()>=2 && ((static_cast<unsigned char>(bytes[0])==0xff && static_cast<unsigned char>(bytes[1])==0xfe)  77         || (static_cast<unsigned char>(bytes[0])==0xfe && static_cast<unsigned char>(bytes[1])==0xff))) {  78         if(!compatible) throw ObjParseError("UTF-16 BOM requires the compatible text reader.");  79         if(bytes.size()%2) throw ObjParseError("Truncated UTF-16 text.");  80         const bool little=static_cast<unsigned char>(bytes[0])==0xff;  81         auto unit=[&](std::size_t i) {  82             const auto a=static_cast<unsigned char>(bytes[i]),b=static_cast<unsigned char>(bytes[i+1]);  83             return unsigned(little?(a|(b<<8)):((a<<8)|b));  84         };  85         text.clear();  86         for(std::size_t i=2;i<bytes.size();i+=2) {  87             unsigned c=unit(i);  88             if(c>=0xd800 && c<=0xdbff) {  89                 if(i+3>=bytes.size()) throw ObjParseError("Truncated UTF-16 surrogate pair.");  90                 const auto low=unit(i+2);  91                 if(low<0xdc00 || low>0xdfff) throw ObjParseError("Invalid UTF-16 surrogate pair.");  92                 c=0x10000+((c-0xd800)<<10)+(low-0xdc00); i+=2;  93             } else if(c>=0xdc00 && c<=0xdfff) throw ObjParseError("Unpaired UTF-16 surrogate.");  94             if(c<0x80) text+=char(c);  95             else if(c<0x800) { text+=char(0xc0|(c>>6)); text+=char(0x80|(c&63)); }  96             else if(c<0x10000) { text+=char(0xe0|(c>>12)); text+=char(0x80|((c>>6)&63)); text+=char(0x80|(c&63)); }  97             else { text+=char(0xf0|(c>>18)); text+=char(0x80|((c>>12)&63)); text+=char(0x80|((c>>6)&63)); text+=char(0x80|(c&63)); }  98         }  99         objWarning(warnings,"Decoded UTF-16 BOM text as UTF-8."); 100     } 101     if(text.compare(0,3,"\xEF\xBB\xBF")==0) text.erase(0,3); 102     if(text.find('\0')!=std::string::npos) throw ObjParseError("NUL byte in OBJ text; unsupported encoding or binary file."); 103     std::string lines; 104     for(std::size_t i=0;i<text.size();++i) { 105         if(text[i]=='\r') { lines+='\n'; if(i+1<text.size() && text[i+1]=='\n') ++i; } 106         else lines+=text[i]; 107     } 108     return lines; 109 } 110 inline Model parseObj(const std::string& bytes,const std::string& name,const LoadOptions& options, 111                       bool compatible,std::vector<std::string>& warnings) { 112     std::istringstream input(objText(bytes,compatible,warnings)); 113     std::vector<glm::vec3> positions,normals; 114     std::vector<glm::vec2> uvs; 115     std::vector<GlColor> colors; 116     std::vector<ObjFace> faces; 117     bool hasColors=false; 118     std::string physical,line,group="face"; 119     std::size_t lineNumber=0,start=0,batch=0; 120     while(std::getline(input,physical)) { 121         ++lineNumber; 122         if(line.empty()) start=lineNumber; 123         physical=physical.substr(0,physical.find('#')); 124         const auto end=physical.find_last_not_of(" \t"); 125         const bool continued=end!=std::string::npos && physical[end]=='\\'; 126         line+=continued?physical.substr(0,end):physical; 127         if(continued) { line+=' '; continue; } 128         std::istringstream row(line); row.imbue(std::locale::classic()); 129         std::string kind; row>>kind; 130         try { 131             if(kind=="v") { 132                 const auto values=objNumbers(row); 133                 if(values.size()!=3 && values.size()!=4 && values.size()!=6) 134                     throw ObjParseError("Expected v x y z [w] or v x y z r g b."); 135                 glm::vec3 p(values[0],values[1],values[2]); 136                 if(values.size()==4) { 137                     if(values[3]==0) throw ObjParseError("Homogeneous vertex has zero w."); 138                     p/=values[3]; 139                 } 140                 if(!finiteVector(p)) throw ObjParseError("Vertex overflows after division by w."); 141                 positions.push_back(p); 142                 colors.push_back(values.size()==6?GlColor{values[3],values[4],values[5]}:GlColor{}); 143                 hasColors=hasColors || values.size()==6; 144             } else if(kind=="vt") { 145                 const auto values=objNumbers(row); 146                 if(values.empty() || values.size()>3 || (values.size()==3 && values[2]!=0)) 147                     throw ObjParseError("Expected vt u [v [0]]; volume texture coordinates are unsupported."); 148                 uvs.push_back({values[0],values.size()>1?values[1]:0}); 149             } else if(kind=="vn") { 150                 const auto values=objNumbers(row); 151                 if(values.size()!=3) throw ObjParseError("Expected vn x y z."); 152                 const glm::dvec3 normal(values[0],values[1],values[2]); 153                 if(glm::length(normal)==0) throw ObjParseError("Zero OBJ normal."); 154                 normals.push_back(glm::vec3(glm::normalize(normal))); 155             } else if(kind=="f") { 156                 ObjFace face; face.positions=positions.size(); face.uvs=uvs.size(); face.normals=normals.size(); 157                 face.line=start; face.batch=batch; face.name=group; 158                 std::string token; 159                 while(row>>token) face.corners.push_back(objCorner(token)); 160                 if(face.corners.size()<3) throw ObjParseError("Face has fewer than three corners."); 161                 faces.push_back(std::move(face)); 162             } else if(kind=="o" || kind=="g") { 163                 std::getline(row,group); const auto first=group.find_first_not_of(" \t"); 164                 group=first==std::string::npos?"face":group.substr(first); ++batch; 165             } else if(kind=="mtllib" || kind=="usemtl") { 166                 ++batch; 167                 objWarning(warnings,"MTL materials are not loaded; set LoadOptions.texturePath or a Scene material explicitly."); 168             } else if(kind=="s") { 169                 ++batch; 170                 std::string smoothing; row>>smoothing; 171                 if(smoothing!="off" && smoothing!="0") 172                     objWarning(warnings,"Smoothing groups are not interpolated; supplied vn values are preserved and missing normals are generated per face."); 173             } else if(!kind.empty()) throw ObjParseError("Unsupported OBJ record: "+kind); 174         } catch(const ObjParseError& e) { throw ObjParseError("line "+std::to_string(start)+": "+e.what()); } 175         line.clear(); 176     } 177     if(!line.empty()) throw ObjParseError("line "+std::to_string(start)+": unterminated line continuation."); 178     Model model; model.shape=GLShape::Custom; model.name=name; 179     Model segment; std::size_t activeBatch=0; 180     auto flush=[&] { 181         if(options.mergeCoplanarFaces && !hasColors) mergeCoplanar(segment); 182         for(auto& face:segment.faces) model.faces.push_back(std::move(face)); 183         segment.faces.clear(); 184     }; 185     for(auto& face:faces) { 186         try { 187             if(face.batch!=activeBatch) { flush(); activeBatch=face.batch; } 188             if(compatible) { 189                 const auto old=face.corners.size(); 190                 face.corners.erase(std::unique(face.corners.begin(),face.corners.end()),face.corners.end()); 191                 if(face.corners.size()>1 && face.corners.front()==face.corners.back()) face.corners.pop_back(); 192                 if(face.corners.size()!=old) objWarning(warnings,"Removed repeated identical OBJ corners at line "+std::to_string(face.line)+"."); 193             } 194             Mesh mesh; mesh.name=face.name+"_"+std::to_string(face.line); 195             std::size_t uvCount=0; 196             bool forward=false; 197             for(const auto& corner:face.corners) { 198                 const auto p=objResolve(corner.position,face.positions,positions.size(),compatible); 199                 mesh.localVertices.push_back(positions[p]); 200                 if(hasColors) mesh.vertexColors.push_back(colors[p]); 201                 forward=forward || (corner.position>0 && static_cast<unsigned long long>(corner.position)>face.positions); 202                 glm::vec2 uv(0); 203                 if(corner.uv) { 204                     uv=uvs[objResolve(corner.uv,face.uvs,uvs.size(),compatible)]; ++uvCount; 205                     forward=forward || (corner.uv>0 && static_cast<unsigned long long>(corner.uv)>face.uvs); 206                 } 207                 mesh.texcoords.push_back(uv); 208             } 209             // Normalize only the triangulation workspace; retain original coordinates. 210             if(mesh.localVertices.size()<3) throw ObjParseError("Face has fewer than three distinct corners."); 211             glm::dvec3 low(mesh.localVertices[0]),high(low); 212             for(const auto& p:mesh.localVertices) { low=glm::min(low,glm::dvec3(p)); high=glm::max(high,glm::dvec3(p)); } 213             const auto size=high-low; const double scale=std::max({size.x,size.y,size.z}); 214             if(scale==0) throw ObjParseError("Degenerate polygon."); 215             Mesh workspace; 216             for(const auto& p:mesh.localVertices) workspace.localVertices.push_back(glm::vec3((glm::dvec3(p)-low)/scale)); 217             if(workspace.localVertices.size()>3) { 218                 glm::dvec3 plane(0); 219                 const auto count=workspace.localVertices.size(); 220                 for(std::size_t i=0;i<count;++i) { 221                     const auto a=glm::dvec3(workspace.localVertices[i]); 222                     const auto b=glm::dvec3(workspace.localVertices[(i+1)%count]); 223                     plane+=glm::cross(a,b); 224                 } 225                 if(glm::length(plane)<1e-12) throw ObjParseError("Degenerate polygon plane."); 226                 plane=glm::normalize(plane); 227                 const auto absolute=glm::abs(plane); 228                 const int axis=absolute.x>absolute.y?(absolute.x>absolute.z?0:2):(absolute.y>absolute.z?1:2); 229                 std::vector<glm::dvec2> projected; 230                 for(const auto& p:workspace.localVertices) { 231                     if(std::abs(glm::dot(plane,glm::dvec3(p-workspace.localVertices[0])))>1e-5) 232                         throw ObjParseError("Non-planar OBJ polygon; export triangulated faces."); 233                     projected.push_back(axis==0?glm::dvec2(p.y,p.z):axis==1?glm::dvec2(p.x,p.z):glm::dvec2(p.x,p.y)); 234                 } 235                 auto cross=[](glm::dvec2 a,glm::dvec2 b,glm::dvec2 c) { 236                     return (b.x-a.x)*(c.y-a.y)-(b.y-a.y)*(c.x-a.x); 237                 }; 238                 auto on=[](glm::dvec2 a,glm::dvec2 b,glm::dvec2 p) { 239                     return p.x>=std::min(a.x,b.x)-1e-14 && p.x<=std::max(a.x,b.x)+1e-14 240                         && p.y>=std::min(a.y,b.y)-1e-14 && p.y<=std::max(a.y,b.y)+1e-14; 241                 }; 242                 for(std::size_t i=0;i<count;++i) for(std::size_t j=i+1;j<count;++j) { 243                     if((i+1)%count==j || (j+1)%count==i) continue; 244                     const auto a=projected[i],b=projected[(i+1)%count],c=projected[j],d=projected[(j+1)%count]; 245                     const auto abC=cross(a,b,c),abD=cross(a,b,d),cdA=cross(c,d,a),cdB=cross(c,d,b); 246                     const bool crossing=((abC>1e-14 && abD< -1e-14)||(abC< -1e-14 && abD>1e-14)) 247                         && ((cdA>1e-14 && cdB< -1e-14)||(cdA< -1e-14 && cdB>1e-14)); 248                     if(crossing || (std::abs(abC)<=1e-14 && on(a,b,c)) || (std::abs(abD)<=1e-14 && on(a,b,d)) 249                         || (std::abs(cdA)<=1e-14 && on(c,d,a)) || (std::abs(cdB)<=1e-14 && on(c,d,b))) 250                         throw ObjParseError("Self-intersection or repeated corner in OBJ polygon."); 251                 } 252             } 253             try { mesh.triangleIndices=triangulate(workspace.localVertices); } 254             catch(const std::runtime_error& e) { throw ObjParseError(e.what()); } 255             workspace.triangleIndices=mesh.triangleIndices; 256             recalculateNormals(workspace); 257             mesh.vertexNormals=std::move(workspace.vertexNormals); 258             for(std::size_t i=0;i<face.corners.size();++i) if(face.corners[i].normal) { 259                 const auto value=face.corners[i].normal; 260                 mesh.vertexNormals[i]=normals[objResolve(value,face.normals,normals.size(),compatible)]; 261                 forward=forward || (value>0 && static_cast<unsigned long long>(value)>face.normals); 262             } 263             if(forward) objWarning(warnings,"Resolved positive forward references at line "+std::to_string(face.line)+"."); 264             if(uvCount==0) mesh.texcoords.clear(); 265             else if(uvCount!=face.corners.size()) objWarning(warnings,"Filled missing UV corners with (0,0) at line "+std::to_string(face.line)+"."); 266             const auto& indices=mesh.triangleIndices; 267             mesh.normal=glm::normalize(glm::cross(workspace.localVertices[indices[1]]-workspace.localVertices[indices[0]], 268                                                   workspace.localVertices[indices[2]]-workspace.localVertices[indices[0]])); 269             if(!validMesh(mesh)) throw ObjParseError("Invalid or degenerate OBJ mesh."); 270             segment.faces.push_back(std::move(mesh)); 271         } catch(const ObjParseError& e) { throw ObjParseError("line "+std::to_string(face.line)+": "+e.what()); } 272     } 273     flush(); 274     if(options.mergeCoplanarFaces && hasColors) objWarning(warnings,"Coplanar merging is skipped to preserve vertex colors."); 275     if(model.faces.empty()) throw ObjParseError("OBJ contains no polygon faces."); 276     return model; 277 } 278 } // namespace graphics::detail 279  280 namespace graphics { 281 inline ModelResult AssetLoader::loadObj(const std::filesystem::path& relative,const LoadOptions& options) const { 282     ModelResult result; 283     const auto utf8=relative.u8string(); 284     const std::string name(utf8.begin(),utf8.end()); 285     try { 286         if(!options.texturePath.empty() && !features_.textures) 287             throw std::runtime_error("Enable texture mode before specifying texturePath."); 288         std::ifstream input(resolve(relative),std::ios::binary); 289         if(!input) throw std::runtime_error("Cannot open OBJ text."); 290         const std::string bytes((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(input)),std::istreambuf_iterator<char>()); 291         if(input.bad()) throw std::runtime_error("OBJ file read failed."); 292         Model model; 293         try { model=detail::parseObj(bytes,name,options,false,result.warnings); } 294         catch(const detail::ObjParseError& first) { 295             if(!options.allowObjFallback) throw; 296             result.warnings.clear(); 297             try { model=detail::parseObj(bytes,name,options,true,result.warnings); } 298             catch(const detail::ObjParseError& second) { 299                 throw std::runtime_error(std::string("Standard reader: ")+first.what()+"; compatible reader: "+second.what()); 300             } 301             result.usedFallback=true; 302             detail::objWarning(result.warnings,std::string("Compatible OBJ reader used after: ")+first.what()); 303         } 304         if(!options.texturePath.empty()) { 305             auto image=texture(options.texturePath,options.flipTextureRows); 306             for(auto& mesh:model.faces) { 307                 if(mesh.texcoords.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("Explicit texture requires UV coordinates on every face."); 308                 mesh.material.texture=image; mesh.material.useTexture=true; 309             } 310         } 311         auto finished=detail::finishLoad(std::move(model),options); 312         finished.warnings=std::move(result.warnings); finished.usedFallback=result.usedFallback; 313         return finished; 314     } catch(const std::exception& e) { 315         result.error=name+": "+e.what(); 316         std::cerr << "[OBJ load failed] " << result.error << '\n' 317             << "Check the file path and the reported cause first, including texture settings.\n" 318             << "Supported OBJ text format (.obj or .txt), one record per line:\n" 319             << "  v x y z       : vertex position\n" 320             << "  vt u [v]      : optional texture coordinate\n" 321             << "  vn x y z      : optional nonzero normal\n" 322             << "  f 1 2 3       : face using 1-based vertex indices\n" 323             << "  Corner forms: v, v/vt, v//vn, v/vt/vn; negative relative indices are allowed.\n" 324             << "Minimal triangle example:\n" 325             << "  v 0 0 0\n  v 1 0 0\n  v 0 1 0\n  f 1 2 3\n" 326             << "If parsing still fails, inspect the model's source format and write a parser for it.\n" 327             << "Fill graphics::Mesh.localVertices and triangleIndices, then call\n" 328             << "graphics::recalculateNormals(mesh), add the mesh to graphics::Model.faces,\n" 329             << "and pass that model to Scene::addModel. See framework/docs/OBJ_LOADING.md.\n"; 330         return result; 331     } 332 } 333 } // namespace graphics
framework/detail/Patterns.inl
   1 #pragma once   2 #include <algorithm>   3 #include <cmath>   4    5 namespace graphics {   6 inline std::optional<PathPose> PatternSystem::conveyorCornerPose(double distance,const glm::vec3& start,const glm::vec3& pivot,float radius,float endZ) {   7     const double straight=double(pivot.x)-start.x, arc=1.5707963267948966*radius, last=double(endZ)-pivot.z;   8     if(!detail::finiteVector(start)||!detail::finiteVector(pivot)||!std::isfinite(distance)||!std::isfinite(radius)||radius<=0||!std::isfinite(endZ)||straight<=0||last<=0||std::abs(start.z-(pivot.z-radius))>1e-4f) {   9         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"conveyorCornerPose","Invalid XZ conveyor geometry.");return {};  10     }  11     double d=std::fmod(distance,straight+arc+last);if(d<0)d+=straight+arc+last;  12     PathPose p;  13     if(d<straight) {p.position=start+glm::vec3(float(d),0,0);p.tangent={1,0,0};}  14     else if(d<straight+arc) {const float a=float((d-straight)/radius);p.position={pivot.x+radius*std::sin(a),start.y,pivot.z-radius*std::cos(a)};p.tangent={std::cos(a),0,std::sin(a)};}  15     else {p.position={pivot.x+radius,start.y,pivot.z+float(d-straight-arc)};p.tangent={0,0,1};}  16     p.yawDegrees=glm::degrees(std::atan2(p.tangent.x,p.tangent.z));return p;  17 }  18 inline bool Bounds3::valid() const {  19     return detail::finiteVector(minimum) && detail::finiteVector(maximum)  20         && maximum.x>minimum.x && maximum.y>minimum.y && maximum.z>minimum.z;  21 }  22 inline bool Bounds3::contains(const glm::vec3& p,const glm::vec3& margin) const {  23     return valid() && detail::finiteVector(p) && detail::finiteVector(margin)  24         && margin.x>=0 && margin.y>=0 && margin.z>=0  25         && p.x>=minimum.x+margin.x && p.x<=maximum.x-margin.x  26         && p.y>=minimum.y+margin.y && p.y<=maximum.y-margin.y  27         && p.z>=minimum.z+margin.z && p.z<=maximum.z-margin.z;  28 }  29 inline bool Bounds3::overlaps(const Bounds3& b) const {  30     return valid() && b.valid() && minimum.x<b.maximum.x && maximum.x>b.minimum.x  31         && minimum.y<b.maximum.y && maximum.y>b.minimum.y && minimum.z<b.maximum.z && maximum.z>b.minimum.z;  32 }  33 inline bool DistancePath::setPoints(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points,bool closed) {  34     if(points.size()<2) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"DistancePath::setPoints","Need at least two points.");  35     auto candidate=points;  36     for(const auto& p:candidate) if(!detail::finiteVector(p))  37         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"DistancePath::setPoints","Path contains NaN/Inf.");  38     if(closed && glm::length(glm::dvec3(candidate.front())-glm::dvec3(candidate.back()))>1e-10) candidate.push_back(candidate.front());  39     std::vector<double> distances{0};  40     for(std::size_t i=1;i<candidate.size();++i) {  41         const double segment=glm::length(glm::dvec3(candidate[i])-glm::dvec3(candidate[i-1]));  42         if(!std::isfinite(segment) || segment<=1e-10 || !std::isfinite(distances.back()+segment))  43             return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"DistancePath::setPoints","Zero-length, repeated or overflowing path segment.");  44         distances.push_back(distances.back()+segment);  45     }  46     points_=std::move(candidate);distances_=std::move(distances);return true;  47 }  48 inline std::optional<PathPose> DistancePath::sample(double distance,Playback playback) const {  49     const double total=length();  50     if(total<=0 || !std::isfinite(distance)) {  51         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"DistancePath::sample","Configure a nonempty path and finite distance.");return {};  52     }  53     double sign=1;  54     if(playback==Playback::Once) distance=std::clamp(distance,0.0,total);  55     else if(playback==Playback::Loop) { distance=std::fmod(distance,total);if(distance<0) distance+=total; }  56     else if(playback==Playback::PingPong) {  57         distance=std::fmod(distance,2*total);if(distance<0) distance+=2*total;  58         if(distance>total) {distance=2*total-distance;sign=-1;}  59     } else { Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"DistancePath::sample","Invalid Playback value.");return {}; }  60     const auto end=std::upper_bound(distances_.begin(),distances_.end(),distance);  61     const std::size_t i=end==distances_.end()?distances_.size()-1:std::size_t(end-distances_.begin());  62     const float weight=float((distance-distances_[i-1])/(distances_[i]-distances_[i-1]));  63     PathPose pose;pose.position=glm::mix(points_[i-1],points_[i],weight);  64     pose.tangent=glm::vec3(glm::normalize(glm::dvec3(points_[i])-glm::dvec3(points_[i-1]))*sign);  65     pose.yawDegrees=glm::degrees(std::atan2(pose.tangent.x,pose.tangent.z));return pose;  66 }  67 inline bool AnimationClock::advance(double dt) {  68     if(!std::isfinite(dt) || dt<0 || !std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds<0 || !std::isfinite(seconds+dt))  69         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"AnimationClock::advance","Time must be finite, nonnegative and within range.");  70     if(playing) seconds+=dt;return true;  71 }  72 namespace detail {  73 inline bool rigidRoom(const glm::mat4& matrix) {  74     const auto r=glm::mat3(matrix), identity=glm::transpose(r)*r;  75     return TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix) && std::abs(glm::determinant(r)-1)<.001f  76         && glm::length(identity[0]-glm::vec3(1,0,0))<.001f  77         && glm::length(identity[1]-glm::vec3(0,1,0))<.001f  78         && glm::length(identity[2]-glm::vec3(0,0,1))<.001f;  79 }  80 inline bool horizontalOverlap(const CharacterBody& b,const glm::vec3& feet,const Bounds3& box) {  81     return feet.x+b.halfWidth.x>box.minimum.x && feet.x-b.halfWidth.x<box.maximum.x  82         && feet.z+b.halfWidth.y>box.minimum.z && feet.z-b.halfWidth.y<box.maximum.z;  83 }  84 }  85 inline bool CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(RoomBody& body,const Bounds3& room,const glm::mat4& roomWorld,  86         float dt,bool floorOpen,bool elastic,const glm::vec3& gravity,const glm::vec3& angularVelocity) {  87     if(!room.valid() || !detail::rigidRoom(roomWorld) || !detail::finiteVector(gravity)  88         || !detail::finiteVector(angularVelocity) || !detail::finiteVector(body.localPosition)  89         || !detail::finiteVector(body.localVelocity) || !detail::positiveLengths(body.halfExtent)  90         || !detail::finiteVector(body.worldPosition) || !detail::finiteVector(body.worldVelocity)  91         || !std::isfinite(dt) || dt<0 || dt>.25f) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody",  92             "Need a rigid room transform, finite body state, positive half extents and dt in [0,.25].");  93     const auto low=room.minimum+body.halfExtent, high=room.maximum-body.halfExtent;  94     if(low.x>=high.x || low.y>=high.y || low.z>=high.z)  95         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody","Body does not fit inside the room.");  96     auto next=body;  97     const int steps=std::max(1,int(std::ceil(dt*120)));const float h=dt/steps;  98     const auto rotation=glm::mat3(roomWorld);  99     const auto localGravity=glm::transpose(rotation)*gravity; 100     for(int s=0;s<steps;++s) { 101         if(next.released) {next.worldVelocity+=gravity*h;next.worldPosition+=next.worldVelocity*h;continue;} 102         if(!elastic) next.localVelocity+=localGravity*h; 103         next.localPosition+=next.localVelocity*h; 104         if(floorOpen && next.localPosition.y<low.y) { 105             next.released=true;next.worldPosition=glm::vec3(roomWorld*glm::vec4(next.localPosition,1)); 106             next.worldVelocity=rotation*next.localVelocity+glm::cross(angularVelocity,next.worldPosition-glm::vec3(roomWorld[3]));continue; 107         } 108         for(int axis=0;axis<3;++axis) { 109             if(elastic && !(floorOpen && axis==1)) { 110                 const double width=double(high[axis])-low[axis]; 111                 const double p=double(next.localPosition[axis])-low[axis]; 112                 double folded=std::fmod(p,2*width);if(folded<0) folded+=2*width; 113                 const bool reflected=folded>width; 114                 next.localPosition[axis]=float(low[axis]+(reflected?2*width-folded:folded)); 115                 if(reflected) next.localVelocity[axis]=-next.localVelocity[axis]; 116                 if(next.localPosition[axis]<=low[axis]) next.localVelocity[axis]=std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]); 117                 if(next.localPosition[axis]>=high[axis]) next.localVelocity[axis]=-std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]); 118             } else { 119                 if(next.localPosition[axis]<low[axis] && !(floorOpen && axis==1)) { 120                     next.localPosition[axis]=low[axis];next.localVelocity[axis]=elastic?std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]):std::max(0.0f,next.localVelocity[axis]); 121                 } 122                 if(next.localPosition[axis]>high[axis]) { 123                     next.localPosition[axis]=high[axis];next.localVelocity[axis]=elastic?-std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]):std::min(0.0f,next.localVelocity[axis]); 124                 } 125             } 126         } 127     } 128     if(!detail::finiteVector(next.localPosition) || !detail::finiteVector(next.localVelocity) 129         || !detail::finiteVector(next.worldPosition) || !detail::finiteVector(next.worldVelocity)) 130         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody","Simulation overflow; original state was preserved."); 131     body=next;return true; 132 } 133 inline bool CollisionSystem::jump(CharacterBody& body,float speed) { 134     if(!std::isfinite(speed) || speed<=0) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::jump","Jump speed must be positive and finite."); 135     if(!body.grounded) return false; // Normal input rejection, not an API error. 136     body.verticalSpeed=speed;body.grounded=false;return true; 137 } 138 inline std::optional<CharacterStep> CollisionSystem::stepCharacter(CharacterBody& body,const Bounds3& stage, 139         const std::vector<Bounds3>& obstacles,float dt,float gravity) { 140     if(!stage.valid() || !detail::finiteVector(body.feet) || !detail::finiteVector(body.direction) 141         || !std::isfinite(body.speed) || body.speed<0 || !std::isfinite(body.height) || body.height<=0 142         || !std::isfinite(body.halfWidth.x) || !std::isfinite(body.halfWidth.y) || body.halfWidth.x<=0 || body.halfWidth.y<=0 143         || !std::isfinite(body.verticalSpeed) || !std::isfinite(body.walkDistance) 144         || !std::isfinite(dt) || dt<0 || dt>.25f || !std::isfinite(gravity) || gravity<=0) { 145         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepCharacter","Invalid bounds, body dimensions, speed, gravity or dt.");return {}; 146     } 147     if(stage.maximum.x-stage.minimum.x<=2*body.halfWidth.x || stage.maximum.z-stage.minimum.z<=2*body.halfWidth.y) { 148         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepCharacter","Character does not fit on the stage.");return {}; 149     } 150     float spacing=std::min(body.halfWidth.x,body.halfWidth.y)*.5f; 151     for(const auto& box:obstacles) { 152         if(!box.valid()) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepCharacter","Invalid obstacle bounds.");return {};} 153         spacing=std::min(spacing,std::min(box.maximum.x-box.minimum.x,box.maximum.z-box.minimum.z)*.5f); 154     } 155     const double count=std::ceil(std::max(double(dt)*120,double(body.speed)*dt/std::max(double(spacing),1e-9))); 156     if(count>4096) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepCharacter","Motion exceeds the substep budget; reduce speed or dt.");return {};} 157     auto next=body;CharacterStep event;next.direction.y=0; 158     const double directionLength=glm::length(glm::dvec3(next.direction)); 159     next.direction=directionLength>1e-10?glm::vec3(glm::dvec3(next.direction)/directionLength):glm::vec3(0); 160     const int steps=std::max(1,int(count));const float h=dt/steps; 161     for(int i=0;i<steps;++i) { 162         const auto previous=next.feet;auto candidate=previous+next.direction*(next.speed*h); 163         bool blocked=candidate.x-next.halfWidth.x<stage.minimum.x || candidate.x+next.halfWidth.x>stage.maximum.x 164             || candidate.z-next.halfWidth.y<stage.minimum.z || candidate.z+next.halfWidth.y>stage.maximum.z; 165         for(const auto& box:obstacles) if(previous.y<box.maximum.y-1e-4f && previous.y+next.height>box.minimum.y+1e-4f 166             && detail::horizontalOverlap(next,candidate,box)) blocked=true; 167         if(blocked) {next.direction=-next.direction;candidate=previous;event.blocked=event.turned=true;} 168         next.feet=candidate;next.walkDistance+=glm::length(glm::dvec2(candidate.x-previous.x,candidate.z-previous.z)); 169         bool supported=std::abs(next.feet.y-stage.minimum.y)<1e-4f; 170         for(const auto& box:obstacles) if(detail::horizontalOverlap(next,next.feet,box) && std::abs(next.feet.y-box.maximum.y)<1e-4f) supported=true; 171         if(next.grounded && !supported) next.grounded=false; 172         if(!next.grounded) { 173             const float oldY=next.feet.y;next.verticalSpeed-=gravity*h;float newY=oldY+next.verticalSpeed*h; 174             float landing=stage.minimum.y; 175             for(const auto& box:obstacles) if(detail::horizontalOverlap(next,next.feet,box)) { 176                 if(next.verticalSpeed<=0 && oldY>=box.maximum.y-1e-4f && newY<=box.maximum.y) landing=std::max(landing,box.maximum.y); 177                 if(next.verticalSpeed>0 && oldY+next.height<=box.minimum.y && newY+next.height>=box.minimum.y) { 178                     newY=box.minimum.y-next.height;next.verticalSpeed=0; 179                 } 180             } 181             if(next.verticalSpeed<=0 && newY<=landing && oldY>=landing-1e-4f) { 182                 newY=landing;next.verticalSpeed=0;next.grounded=true;event.landed=true; 183             } 184             next.feet.y=newY; 185         } 186     } 187     if(!detail::finiteVector(next.feet) || !std::isfinite(next.walkDistance) || !std::isfinite(next.verticalSpeed)) { 188         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"CollisionSystem::stepCharacter","Simulation overflow; original state was preserved.");return {}; 189     } 190     body=next;return event; 191 } 192 inline std::optional<Bounds3> CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox(const Bounds3& area,const glm::vec3& size, 193         const std::vector<Bounds3>& occupied,std::mt19937& random,unsigned attempts) { 194     if(!area.valid() || !detail::positiveLengths(size) || attempts==0 || attempts>100000 195         || size.x>area.maximum.x-area.minimum.x || size.y>area.maximum.y-area.minimum.y || size.z>area.maximum.z-area.minimum.z) { 196         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox","Box must fit in the area; attempts must be in [1,100000].");return {}; 197     } 198     for(const auto& box:occupied) if(!box.valid()) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox","Invalid occupied bounds.");return {};} 199     for(unsigned i=0;i<attempts;++i) { 200         Bounds3 box; 201         for(int axis=0;axis<3;++axis) box.minimum[axis]=std::uniform_real_distribution<float>(area.minimum[axis],area.maximum[axis]-size[axis])(random); 202         box.maximum=box.minimum+size; 203         if(std::none_of(occupied.begin(),occupied.end(),[&](const Bounds3& other){return box.overlaps(other);})) return box; 204     } 205     Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox","No free placement within the attempt budget.","Use smaller objects, fewer occupied regions or a larger placement area.");return {}; 206 } 207 inline std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addBox(Scene& scene,const glm::vec3& size,const glm::vec3& position, 208         GlColor color,std::optional<EntityId> parent) { 209     if(!detail::positiveLengths(size) || !detail::finiteVector(position) || !detail::validColor(color) || (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent))) { 210         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::addBox","Need positive dimensions, finite position/color and an existing parent.");return {}; 211     } 212     auto model=DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube,size);for(auto& face:model.faces) face.color=color; 213     Transform3D transform;transform.basis=position; 214     const auto entity=scene.addModel(model,transform,PivotMode::Preserve); 215     if(!entity || !scene.setParent(*entity,parent)) return {};return entity; 216 } 217 inline std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addPolygon(Scene& scene,const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points, 218         GlColor color,std::optional<EntityId> parent) { 219     if(points.size()<3 || !detail::validColor(color) || (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent))) { 220         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::addPolygon","Need a convex polygon (>=3 points), valid color and parent.");return {}; 221     } 222     Mesh face;face.localVertices=points;face.color=color; 223     for(std::size_t i=1;i+1<points.size();++i) face.triangleIndices.insert(face.triangleIndices.end(),{0,std::uint32_t(i),std::uint32_t(i+1)}); 224     recalculateNormals(face);Model model;model.faces.push_back(face); 225     const auto entity=scene.addModel(model,Transform3D{},PivotMode::Preserve); 226     if(!entity || !scene.setParent(*entity,parent)) return {};return entity; 227 } 228 inline std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addRoom(Scene& scene,const Bounds3& bounds,bool frontOpen) { 229     if(!bounds.valid()) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::addRoom","Room bounds must have positive finite extents.");return {};} 230     auto model=DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube,bounds.maximum-bounds.minimum); 231     for(auto& face:model.faces) {face.cull=CullMode::Front;recalculateNormals(face);for(auto& normal:face.vertexNormals) normal=-normal;} 232     model.faces[0].visible=!frontOpen; 233     Transform3D transform;transform.basis=(bounds.minimum+bounds.maximum)*.5f; 234     return scene.addModel(model,transform,PivotMode::Preserve); 235 } 236 inline std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addJoint(Scene& scene,EntityId parent,const glm::vec3& anchor) { 237     if(!scene.getComponent(parent) || !detail::finiteVector(anchor)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::addJoint","Missing parent or non-finite anchor.");return {};} 238     Transform3D transform;transform.basis=anchor;const auto joint=scene.addGroup(transform); 239     if(!joint || !scene.setParent(*joint,parent)) return {};return joint; 240 } 241 inline bool PatternSystem::rotateJoint(Scene& scene,EntityId joint,const glm::vec3& axis,float degrees) { 242     const auto* component=scene.getComponent(joint);const auto rotation=TransformSystem::rotation({{0,0,0},axis},degrees);
