245 } 246 inline bool PatternSystem::follow(Scene& scene,EntityId entity,const DistancePath& path,double distance,const glm::vec3& offset,bool orient) { 247     const auto pose=path.sample(distance);const auto* component=scene.getComponent(entity); 248     if(!pose || !component || !detail::finiteVector(offset)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"PatternSystem::follow","Invalid path sample, entity or offset."); 249     auto transform=component->transform;transform.basis=pose->position+offset; 250     if(orient) transform.rotation=glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),glm::radians(pose->yawDegrees),glm::vec3(0,1,0))); 251     return scene.setTransform(entity,transform); 252 } 253 inline bool PatternSystem::addNumber(Scene& scene,unsigned digit,const glm::vec3& position,float height,GlColor color,std::optional<EntityId> parent) { 254     if(digit>9 || !detail::finiteVector(position) || !std::isfinite(height) || height<=0 || !detail::validColor(color) || (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent))) 255         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::addNumber","Need digit 0-9, positive height, finite values and a valid parent."); 256     constexpr unsigned masks[10]={0x3f,0x06,0x5b,0x4f,0x66,0x6d,0x7d,0x07,0x7f,0x6f}; 257     const glm::vec2 endpoints[7][2]={{{0,1},{.5f,1}},{{.5f,1},{.5f,.5f}},{{.5f,.5f},{.5f,0}},{{0,0},{.5f,0}},{{0,.5f},{0,0}},{{0,1},{0,.5f}},{{0,.5f},{.5f,.5f}}}; 258     for(unsigned i=0;i<7;++i) if(masks[digit]&(1u<<i)) { 259         const auto a=position+glm::vec3(endpoints[i][0]*height,0),b=position+glm::vec3(endpoints[i][1]*height,0); 260         if(!scene.addLine(a,b,color,parent)) return false; 261     } 262     return true; 263 } 264 inline bool PatternSystem::foldFace(Scene& scene,EntityId entity,std::size_t face,const AxisLine& hinge,float degrees) { 265     const auto matrix=TransformSystem::rotation(hinge,degrees);return matrix && scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(entity,face,*matrix); 266 } 267 inline bool PatternSystem::openFace(Scene& scene,EntityId entity,std::size_t face,float progress, 268         const glm::vec3& displacement,const glm::vec3& topPivot,bool scaleOpen) { 269     if(!std::isfinite(progress) || progress<0 || progress>1 || !detail::finiteVector(displacement) || !detail::finiteVector(topPivot)) 270         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::openFace","Need progress in [0,1] and finite vectors."); 271     if(scaleOpen) { 272         // Fully open faces are hidden; retain a nonsingular matrix for normals/picking. 273         const auto scale=TransformSystem::scaling({1,std::max(1e-4f,1-progress),1},topPivot); 274         return scale && scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(entity,face,*scale) && scene.setFaceVisible(entity,face,progress<1); 275     } 276     return scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(entity,face,glm::translate(glm::mat4(1),displacement*progress)) && scene.setFaceVisible(entity,face,true); 277 } 278 inline std::optional<std::size_t> PatternSystem::cameraFacingWall(const Scene& scene,EntityId room,const Camera& camera) { 279     const auto* component=scene.getComponent(room);const auto world=scene.worldMatrix(room); 280     if(!component || component->polyhedron.faces.size()!=6 || !world || !CameraSystem::validCamera(camera) 281         || std::abs(glm::determinant(glm::mat3(*world)))<1e-8f) { 282         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"cameraFacingWall","Need a six-face room, invertible world transform and valid camera.");return {}; 283     } 284     const auto view=glm::normalize(camera.target-camera.position); 285     const auto normals=glm::transpose(glm::inverse(glm::mat3(*world))); 286     float best=0;std::optional<std::size_t> selected; 287     for(std::size_t i=0;i<4;++i) { 288         const auto& vertices=component->polyhedron.faces[i].localVertices; 289         if(vertices.size()<3) return {}; 290         const auto n=glm::normalize(normals*glm::cross(vertices[1]-vertices[0],vertices[2]-vertices[0])); 291         const float score=glm::dot(n,view); 292         if(score>best+1e-6f) {best=score;selected=i;} 293     } 294     return selected; // Select the far interior wall visible along the viewing direction. 295 } 296 inline bool PatternSystem::openRoomWall(Scene& scene,EntityId room,std::size_t face,float progress) { 297     const auto* mesh=scene.getMesh(room,face); 298     if(!mesh || face>=4 || mesh->localVertices.empty()) 299         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"openRoomWall","Select one of the four side walls of addRoom()."); 300     glm::vec3 pivot(0);float top=mesh->localVertices.front().y; 301     for(const auto& vertex:mesh->localVertices) {pivot+=vertex;top=std::max(top,vertex.y);} 302     pivot/=float(mesh->localVertices.size());pivot.y=top; 303     return openFace(scene,room,face,progress,glm::vec3(0),pivot,true); 304 } 305 inline bool PatternSystem::swapModels(Scene& scene,EntityId first,EntityId second) { 306     const auto* a=scene.getComponent(first);const auto* b=scene.getComponent(second); 307     if(!a || !b || a->transformOnly || b->transformOnly) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::swapModels","Both entities must contain a model."); 308     const auto firstModel=a->polyhedron,secondModel=b->polyhedron; 309     if(!scene.setModel(first,secondModel)) return false; 310     if(!scene.setModel(second,firstModel)) {scene.setModel(first,firstModel);return false;} 311     return true; 312 } 313 inline std::vector<glm::ivec3> PatternSystem::snakeCells(const glm::ivec3& cells) { 314     std::vector<glm::ivec3> result; 315     if(cells.x<=0 || cells.y!=1 || cells.z<=0 || std::uint64_t(cells.x)*cells.z>1000000) { 316         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"PatternSystem::snakeCells","Need an XZ grid with y=1 and at most 1,000,000 cells.");return result; 317     } 318     for(int z=0;z<cells.z;++z) for(int x=0;x<cells.x;++x) result.push_back({z%2?cells.x-1-x:x,0,z});return result; 319 } 320 inline bool PatternSystem::aligned(float first,float second,float tolerance) { 321     if(!std::isfinite(first) || !std::isfinite(second) || !std::isfinite(tolerance) || tolerance<0) 322         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"PatternSystem::aligned","Need finite heights and nonnegative tolerance."); 323     return std::abs(double(first)-second)<=tolerance; 324 } 325 inline glm::vec3 PatternSystem::snap(const glm::vec3& point,float spacing) { 326     if(!detail::finiteVector(point) || !std::isfinite(spacing) || spacing<=0) { 327         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::snap","Need finite point and positive spacing.");return point; 328     } 329     const auto result=glm::vec3(glm::round(glm::dvec3(point)/double(spacing))*double(spacing)); 330     if(!detail::finiteVector(result)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::snap","Snap overflow.");return point;}return result; 331 } 332 inline std::optional<glm::vec3> PatternSystem::screenOnPlane(const Camera& camera,double x,double y,const PickViewport& viewport, 333         const glm::vec3& planePoint,const glm::vec3& normal) { 334     const auto ray=screenRay(camera,x,y,viewport);const auto n=detail::motionUnitVector(normal); 335     if(!ray || !n || !detail::finiteVector(planePoint)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Input,"PatternSystem::screenOnPlane","Invalid camera, viewport, pointer or plane.");return {};} 336     const double denominator=glm::dot(glm::dvec3(ray->direction),glm::dvec3(*n)); 337     if(std::abs(denominator)<1e-10) return {}; 338     const double t=glm::dot(glm::dvec3(planePoint)-glm::dvec3(ray->origin),glm::dvec3(*n))/denominator; 339     if(t<0 || t>ray->maxDistance) return {};return glm::vec3(glm::dvec3(ray->origin)+glm::dvec3(ray->direction)*t); 340 } 341 inline bool PatternSystem::moveCamera(Camera& camera,const glm::vec3& offset) { 342     auto next=camera;next.position+=offset;next.target+=offset; 343     if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(next)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::moveCamera","Camera translation creates an invalid camera."); 344     camera=next;return true; 345 } 346 inline bool PatternSystem::yawCamera(Camera& camera,float degrees) { 347     if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(camera)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::yawCamera","Invalid camera."); 348     const auto forward=glm::normalize(camera.target-camera.position),right=glm::normalize(glm::cross(forward,camera.up)); 349     const auto up=glm::normalize(glm::cross(right,forward));const auto rotation=TransformSystem::rotation({camera.position,up},degrees); 350     if(!rotation) return false; 351     auto next=camera;next.target=glm::vec3(*rotation*glm::vec4(camera.target,1));next.up=glm::mat3(*rotation)*up; 352     if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(next)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::yawCamera","Rotation creates an invalid camera."); 353     camera=next;return true; 354 } 355 inline bool PatternSystem::orbitCamera(Camera& camera,const AxisLine& axis,float degrees) { 356     const auto rotation=TransformSystem::rotation(axis,degrees);if(!rotation) return false; 357     auto next=camera;next.position=glm::vec3(*rotation*glm::vec4(camera.position,1)); 358     next.target=glm::vec3(*rotation*glm::vec4(camera.target,1));next.up=glm::mat3(*rotation)*camera.up; 359     if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(next)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::orbitCamera","Orbit creates an invalid camera."); 360     camera=next;return true; 361 } 362 inline bool PatternSystem::zoomCamera(Camera& camera,float factor) { 363     if(!std::isfinite(factor) || factor<=0) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::zoomCamera","Zoom factor must be positive and finite."); 364     auto next=camera; 365     if(next.orthographic) next.orthographicHalfExtent*=factor; 366     else next.position=next.target+(next.position-next.target)*factor; 367     if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(next)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"PatternSystem::zoomCamera","Zoom creates an invalid camera."); 368     camera=next;return true; 369 } 370 inline std::vector<RenderPass> PatternSystem::threeViews(const Camera& perspective,int width,int height,const RenderOptions& options) { 371     if(width<3 || height<2 || !CameraSystem::validCamera(perspective)) { 372         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"PatternSystem::threeViews","Need a valid camera and framebuffer of at least 3x2 pixels.");return {}; 373     } 374     const int left=width*2/3,right=width-left,bottom=height/2; 375     RenderPass main;main.camera=perspective;main.camera.orthographic=false;main.options=options;main.viewport={0,0,left,height}; 376     RenderPass top=main;top.viewport={left,bottom,right,height-bottom};top.camera.orthographic=true; 377     top.camera.position=perspective.target+glm::vec3(0,20,0);top.camera.target=perspective.target;top.camera.up={0,0,-1}; 378     top.camera.orthographicHalfExtent=std::max(6.5f,perspective.orthographicHalfExtent); 379     RenderPass front=top;front.viewport={left,0,right,bottom}; 380     front.camera.position=perspective.target+glm::vec3(0,0,20);front.camera.up={0,1,0}; 381     return {main,top,front}; 382 } 383 inline std::optional<JointRig> PatternSystem::addWalkingRig(Scene& scene,const GridLayout& grid) { 384     if(!grid.valid() || !detail::rigidRoom(grid.frame)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::addWalkingRig","Need a valid grid with a rigid frame.");return {};} 385     JointRig rig;const auto root=scene.addGroup();if(!root) return {};rig.root=*root; 386     auto point=[&](glm::vec3 ratio){return grid.origin+grid.extent*ratio;}; 387     const auto torso=addBox(scene,grid.extent*glm::vec3(.45f,.35f,.7f),point({.5f,.58f,.5f}),{.65f,.65f,.7f},rig.root); 388     const auto head=addJoint(scene,rig.root,point({.5f,.8f,.5f}));if(!torso || !head) return {};rig.head=*head; 389     if(!addBox(scene,grid.extent*glm::vec3(.3f,.2f,.6f),{0,grid.extent.y*.1f,0},{1,.8f,.5f},rig.head) 390         || !addBox(scene,grid.extent*glm::vec3(.1f,.05f,.2f),{0,grid.extent.y*.1f,grid.extent.z*.4f},{1,.2f,.2f},rig.head)) return {}; 391     for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) { 392         const float x=i==0?.2f:.8f;const GlColor color=i==0?GlColor{.2f,.6f,1}:GlColor{1,.4f,.2f}; 393         const auto shoulder=addJoint(scene,rig.root,point({x,.72f,.5f})),hip=addJoint(scene,rig.root,point({i==0?.35f:.65f,.4f,.5f})); 394         if(!shoulder || !hip) return {};rig.shoulders[i]=*shoulder;rig.hips[i]=*hip; 395         if(!addBox(scene,grid.extent*glm::vec3(.15f,.32f,.3f),{0,-grid.extent.y*.16f,0},color,*shoulder) 396             || !addBox(scene,grid.extent*glm::vec3(.18f,.4f,.4f),{0,-grid.extent.y*.2f,0},color,*hip)) return {}; 397     } 398     Transform3D transform;transform.rotation=glm::mat3(grid.frame);transform.basis=glm::vec3(grid.frame[3]); 399     if(!scene.setTransform(rig.root,transform)) return {};return rig; 400 } 401 inline bool PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig(Scene& scene,const JointRig& rig,const glm::vec3& feet,float yawDegrees,double distance,float speed) { 402     if(!detail::finiteVector(feet) || !std::isfinite(yawDegrees) || !std::isfinite(distance) || !std::isfinite(speed) || speed<0) 403         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion,"PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig","Need finite pose values and nonnegative speed."); 404     const auto* root=scene.getComponent(rig.root); 405     if(!root || !scene.getComponent(rig.head)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig","Rig IDs are missing or stale."); 406     for(auto id:rig.shoulders) if(!scene.getComponent(id)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig","Shoulder joint is missing."); 407     for(auto id:rig.hips) if(!scene.getComponent(id)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene,"PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig","Hip joint is missing."); 408     auto transform=root->transform;transform.basis=feet; 409     transform.rotation=glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),glm::radians(yawDegrees),glm::vec3(0,1,0))); 410     if(!scene.setTransform(rig.root,transform)) return false; 411     const float angle=std::min(50.0f,15+speed*12)*float(std::sin(std::remainder(distance,1.0)*6.283185307179586)); 412     for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) { 413         const float sign=i==0?1.0f:-1.0f; 414         if(!rotateJoint(scene,rig.hips[i],{1,0,0},sign*angle) || !rotateJoint(scene,rig.shoulders[i],{1,0,0},-sign*angle)) return false; 415     } 416     return true; 417 } 418 } // namespace graphics
framework/detail/PrimitiveLoader.inl
   1 #pragma once   2 namespace graphics {   3 inline ModelResult AssetLoader::loadPrimitive(const std::filesystem::path& relative,const LoadOptions& options) const {   4     return loadObj(relative,options);   5 }   6 } // namespace graphics
framework/detail/Renderer.inl
   1 #pragma once   2 #include "Shaders.h"   3 #include "../dependencies/glm/glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp"   4 #include <algorithm>   5 #include <fstream>   6 #include <iostream>   7 #include <sstream>   8    9 namespace graphics {  10 inline std::string readTextFile(const std::filesystem::path& path) {  11     std::ifstream input(path,std::ios::binary);  12     if(!input) return {};  13     std::ostringstream buffer;buffer<<input.rdbuf();auto text=buffer.str();  14     if(text.compare(0,3,"\xEF\xBB\xBF")==0) text.erase(0,3);  15     return text;  16 }  17 namespace detail {  18 inline GLuint compileShader(GLenum kind,const std::string& sourceText,const std::filesystem::path& sourcePath) {  19     Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Shader, "compileShader");  20     auto& api=gl();const auto shader=api.CreateShader(kind);  21     if(!shader) { Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glCreateShader", "Shader allocation failed."); return 0; }  22     const char* text=sourceText.c_str();api.ShaderSource(shader,1,&text,nullptr);api.CompileShader(shader);  23     GLint success=0;api.GetShaderiv(shader,GL_COMPILE_STATUS,&success);  24     if(!success) {  25         GLint length=0;api.GetShaderiv(shader,GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH,&length);  26         std::string log(std::max(length,1), '\0');api.GetShaderInfoLog(shader,GLsizei(log.size()),nullptr,log.data());  27         Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, kind==GL_VERTEX_SHADER?"vertex shader":"fragment shader", "GLSL file="+sourcePath.u8string()+"\n"+log,  28             "Read the compiler line number; check GLSL version, declarations and shader inputs/outputs.");  29         api.DeleteShader(shader);return 0;  30     }  31     return shader;  32 }  33 struct GpuVertex { float position[3], color[3], uv[2], normal[3]; };  34 inline GLuint uploadTexture(const TextureImage& image,bool repeat) {  35     auto& api=gl();GLuint id=0;api.GenTextures(1,&id);  36     if(!id){Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset,"glGenTextures","Texture allocation failed.");return 0;}  37     api.ActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);api.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,id);  38     GLint previousAlignment=4;api.GetIntegerv(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,&previousAlignment);  39     api.PixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,1);  40     api.TexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0,GL_RGBA8,GLsizei(image.width),GLsizei(image.height),0,GL_RGBA,GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE,image.rgba.data());  41     api.PixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,previousAlignment);  42     api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,GL_LINEAR);  43     api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,GL_LINEAR);  44     api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S,repeat?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);  45     api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T,repeat?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);  46     if(!checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset, "uploadTexture")) {api.DeleteTextures(1,&id);return 0;}  47     return id;  48 }  49 } // namespace detail  50   51 inline bool Renderer::initShader(const std::filesystem::path& vertexPath,const std::filesystem::path& fragmentPath) {  52     auto& gl=detail::gl();  53     const auto vertexText=vertexPath.empty()?std::string(detail::vertexShader):readTextFile(vertexPath);  54     const auto fragmentText=fragmentPath.empty()?std::string(detail::fragmentShader):readTextFile(fragmentPath);  55     if(vertexText.empty()||fragmentText.empty()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "Renderer::initShader",  56         "Shader source is empty or unreadable: vertex=" + vertexPath.u8string() + "; fragment=" + fragmentPath.u8string(),  57         "Use embedded shaders (empty shaderDirectory), or copy both GLSL files to the configured project-relative directory.");  58     // /utf-8 /FC encodes __FILE__ as UTF-8, not the Windows ANSI code page.  59     const auto embeddedSource=std::filesystem::u8path(__FILE__).parent_path()/"Shaders.h";  60     const auto vertex=detail::compileShader(GL_VERTEX_SHADER,vertexText,vertexPath.empty()?embeddedSource:vertexPath);if(!vertex)return false;  61     const auto fragment=detail::compileShader(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER,fragmentText,fragmentPath.empty()?embeddedSource:fragmentPath);  62     if(!fragment){gl.DeleteShader(vertex);return false;}  63     shaderProgramID_=gl.CreateProgram();  64     if(!shaderProgramID_){gl.DeleteShader(vertex);gl.DeleteShader(fragment);return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader,"glCreateProgram","Program allocation failed.");}  65     gl.AttachShader(shaderProgramID_,vertex);gl.AttachShader(shaderProgramID_,fragment);gl.LinkProgram(shaderProgramID_);  66     gl.DeleteShader(vertex);gl.DeleteShader(fragment);  67     GLint success=0;gl.GetProgramiv(shaderProgramID_,GL_LINK_STATUS,&success);  68     if(!success) {  69         GLint size=0;gl.GetProgramiv(shaderProgramID_,GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH,&size);  70         std::string log(std::max(size,1),'\0');gl.GetProgramInfoLog(shaderProgramID_,GLsizei(log.size()),nullptr,log.data());  71         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glLinkProgram", log, "Match vertex outputs and fragment inputs; inspect the linker log.");  72     }  73     mvpLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uMVP");  74     modelLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uModel");  75     normalLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uNormalMatrix");  76     textureLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uTexture");  77     hasTextureLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uHasTexture");  78     lightingLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLighting");  79     lightDirectionLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLightDirection");  80     lightColorLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLightColor");  81     overrideLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uOverrideColor");  82     outlineColorLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uOutlineColor");  83     tintLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uTint");  84     return mvpLocation_>=0 || Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glGetUniformLocation", "Required uMVP uniform is missing or optimized away.");  85 }  86 inline bool Renderer::initialize(const std::filesystem::path& vertexPath,const std::filesystem::path& fragmentPath) {  87     detail::gl().load();destroy();  88     if(!initShader(vertexPath,fragmentPath)){destroy();return false;}  89     auto& gl=detail::gl();gl.GenVertexArrays(1,&vao_);gl.GenBuffers(1,&vbo_);gl.GenBuffers(1,&ebo_);  90     if(!vao_||!vbo_||!ebo_){destroy();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::initialize", "VAO/VBO/EBO allocation failed.");}  91     gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,vbo_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,ebo_);  92     using V=detail::GpuVertex;  93     gl.VertexAttribPointer(0,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,position)));  94     gl.VertexAttribPointer(1,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,color)));  95     gl.VertexAttribPointer(2,2,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,uv)));  96     gl.VertexAttribPointer(3,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,normal)));  97     for(GLuint i=0;i<4;++i)gl.EnableVertexAttribArray(i);  98     gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,0);return detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::initialize vertex layout");  99 } 100 inline bool Renderer::upload(const Scene& scene,bool textureMode) { 101     Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::upload"); 102     std::vector<detail::GpuVertex> vertices; 103     std::vector<GLuint> indices;std::vector<DrawRange> ranges; 104     TextureMap stagedTextures;auto& gl=detail::gl(); 105     auto cleanup=[&](){for(const auto& item:stagedTextures)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second);}; 106     try { 107         for(auto id:scene.getIds()) { 108             const auto* c=scene.getComponent(id);if(c&&c->transformOnly)continue; 109             if(!c||!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(c->polyhedron)){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::upload", "Missing component or invalid mesh, indices, colors, UVs or normals.", "Inspect the entity's mesh data before GPU upload.", id);} 110             for(std::size_t faceIndex=0;faceIndex<c->polyhedron.faces.size();++faceIndex) { 111                 const auto& face=c->polyhedron.faces[faceIndex]; 112                 if(vertices.size()+face.localVertices.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLuint>::max()) {cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Vertex count exceeds the GPU index range.");} 113                 const auto first=GLuint(vertices.size()); 114                 Mesh normals=face;if(normals.vertexNormals.empty())recalculateNormals(normals); 115                 for(std::size_t i=0;i<face.localVertices.size();++i) { 116                     const auto p=face.localVertices[i];const auto n=normals.vertexNormals[i]; 117                     auto color=face.vertexColors.empty()?face.color:face.vertexColors[i]; 118                     color.red*=face.material.color.red;color.green*=face.material.color.green;color.blue*=face.material.color.blue; 119                     const auto uv=face.texcoords.empty()?glm::vec2(0):face.texcoords[i]; 120                     vertices.push_back({{p.x,p.y,p.z},{color.red,color.green,color.blue},{uv.x,uv.y},{n.x,n.y,n.z}}); 121                 } 122                 const auto triangles=detail::triangles(face); 123                 const auto outline=meshOutline(face,face.outline==OutlineMode::None?OutlineMode::FeatureEdges:face.outline); 124                 if(triangles.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLsizei>::max()||outline.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLsizei>::max()){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Draw count exceeds GLsizei range.");} 125                 DrawRange range;range.entityId=id;range.faceIndex=faceIndex;range.firstIndex=indices.size();range.count=GLsizei(triangles.size()); 126                 for(auto index:triangles)indices.push_back(first+index); 127                 range.outlineFirstIndex=indices.size();range.outlineCount=GLsizei(outline.size()); 128                 for(auto index:outline)indices.push_back(first+index); 129                 ranges.push_back(range); 130                 if(textureMode&&face.material.useTexture&&stagedTextures.find(face.material.texture)==stagedTextures.end()) { 131                     if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset, "before uploadTexture")){cleanup();return false;} 132                     const auto texture=detail::uploadTexture(*face.material.texture,face.material.repeatTexture); 133                     if(!texture){cleanup();return false;} 134                     try{stagedTextures.emplace(face.material.texture,texture);}catch(...){gl.DeleteTextures(1,&texture);throw;} 135                 } 136             } 137         } 138         for(std::size_t lineIndex=0;lineIndex<scene.getLines().size();++lineIndex) { 139             const auto& line=scene.getLines()[lineIndex]; 140             if(vertices.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLuint>::max()-2){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload lines","Vertex count exceeds the GPU index range.");} 141             const auto first=GLuint(vertices.size()); 142             for(auto p:{line.start,line.end})vertices.push_back({{p.x,p.y,p.z},{line.color.red,line.color.green,line.color.blue},{0,0},{0,0,0}}); 143             DrawRange range;range.lineId=lineIndex;range.firstIndex=indices.size();range.count=2; 144             ranges.push_back(range);indices.insert(indices.end(),{first,first+1}); 145         } 146         if(vertices.size()>std::size_t(std::numeric_limits<GLsizeiptr>::max())/sizeof(detail::GpuVertex) 147             ||indices.size()>std::size_t(std::numeric_limits<GLsizeiptr>::max())/sizeof(GLuint)){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Buffer byte size exceeds GLsizeiptr range.");} 148         if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "before buffer upload")){cleanup();return false;} 149         gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,vbo_); 150         gl.BufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,GLsizeiptr(vertices.size()*sizeof(detail::GpuVertex)),vertices.empty()?nullptr:vertices.data(),GL_STATIC_DRAW); 151         gl.BindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,ebo_); 152         gl.BufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,GLsizeiptr(indices.size()*sizeof(GLuint)),indices.empty()?nullptr:indices.data(),GL_STATIC_DRAW); 153         gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,0); 154         if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "glBufferData")){cleanup();return false;} 155         for(const auto& item:textures_)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second); 156         textures_=std::move(stagedTextures);drawRanges_=std::move(ranges);return true; 157     } catch(...) {cleanup();throw;} 158 } 159 inline bool Renderer::draw(const Scene& scene,const glm::mat4& view,const glm::mat4& projection, 160                            const RenderOptions& options) const { 161     Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw"); 162     if(!detail::finiteMatrix(view)||!detail::finiteMatrix(projection))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw", "View or projection matrix contains NaN/Inf."); 163     for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) { 164         glm::mat4 matrix(1); 165         if(range.lineId) { 166             const auto world=scene.lineWorldMatrix(*range.lineId);if(!world)return false;matrix=*world; 167         } else { 168             const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId); 169             const auto world=scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);if(!world)return false;matrix=*world; 170         } 171         if(!detail::finiteMatrix(matrix)||!detail::finiteMatrix(projection*view*matrix))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw", "World or MVP matrix contains NaN/Inf.", "Check parent transforms, scales and motion samples.", range.entityId); 172     } 173     auto& gl=detail::gl();gl.UseProgram(shaderProgramID_);gl.BindVertexArray(vao_); 174     auto cull=[&](const Mesh* face,const glm::mat4& model) { 175         const auto mode=face && face->cull!=CullMode::Inherit?face->cull:options.cull; 176         gl.FrontFace(glm::determinant(glm::mat3(model))<0?GL_CW:GL_CCW); 177         if(!face || mode==CullMode::None || mode==CullMode::Inherit) gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE); 178         else {gl.Enable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.CullFace(mode==CullMode::Front?GL_FRONT:GL_BACK);} 179     }; 180     gl.ActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);gl.Uniform1i(textureLocation_,0); 181     gl.Uniform3f(lightDirectionLocation_,options.lightDirection.x,options.lightDirection.y,options.lightDirection.z); 182     gl.Uniform3f(lightColorLocation_,options.lightColor.red,options.lightColor.green,options.lightColor.blue); 183     gl.Uniform3f(outlineColorLocation_,options.outlineColor.red,options.outlineColor.green,options.outlineColor.blue); 184     if(options.wireframe && options.depthTest) { 185         // All surfaces write depth first, so a later object's front face hides earlier back edges. 186         gl.ColorMask(GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE); 187         for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) { 188             if(range.lineId)continue; 189             const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId); 190             const auto& face=c->polyhedron.faces[range.faceIndex]; 191             if(!c->visible||!face.visible)continue; 192             const auto model=*scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex); 193             cull(&face,model); 194             const auto matrix=projection*view*model; 195             gl.UniformMatrix4fv(mvpLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(matrix)); 196             gl.DrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES,range.count,GL_UNSIGNED_INT, 197                 reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.firstIndex*sizeof(GLuint))); 198         } 199         gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE); 200     } 201     for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) { 202         glm::mat4 model(1);const Mesh* face=nullptr;GlColor tint; 203         if(range.lineId) { 204             const auto& line=scene.getLines()[*range.lineId]; 205             if(line.owner){const auto* owner=scene.getComponent(*line.owner);if(!owner||!owner->visible)continue;} 206             const auto world=scene.lineWorldMatrix(*range.lineId);if(!world)return false;model=*world; 207         } else { 208             const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId);if(!c||!c->visible)continue; 209             face=&c->polyhedron.faces[range.faceIndex];if(!face->visible)continue;tint=c->tint; 210             const auto world=scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);if(!world)return false;model=*world; 211         } 212         const auto mvp=projection*view*model; 213         cull(face,model); 214         const float determinant=glm::determinant(glm::mat3(model)); 215         const auto normalMatrix=std::isfinite(determinant)&&std::abs(determinant)>1e-12f 216             ?glm::transpose(glm::inverse(glm::mat3(model))):glm::mat3(1); 217         gl.UniformMatrix4fv(mvpLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(mvp)); 218         gl.UniformMatrix4fv(modelLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(model)); 219         gl.UniformMatrix3fv(normalLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(normalMatrix)); 220         gl.Uniform3f(tintLocation_,tint.red,tint.green,tint.blue); 221         gl.Uniform1i(lightingLocation_,face&&options.lighting);gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,0); 222         const bool textured=face&&options.textures&&face->material.useTexture; 223         gl.Uniform1i(hasTextureLocation_,textured); 224         if(textured){gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,textures_.at(face->material.texture)); 225             gl.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S,face->material.repeatTexture?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE); 226             gl.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T,face->material.repeatTexture?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);} 227         else gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0); 228         if(!face||!options.wireframe) 229         { 230             gl.DrawElements(face?GL_TRIANGLES:GL_LINES,range.count,GL_UNSIGNED_INT, 231                 reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.firstIndex*sizeof(GLuint))); 232             if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster, "glDrawElements")) { 233                 gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.UseProgram(0);return false; 234             } 235         } 236         if(face&&(options.wireframe||face->outline!=OutlineMode::None)) { 237             gl.Uniform1i(hasTextureLocation_,0);gl.Uniform1i(lightingLocation_,0);gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,options.wireframe?0:1); 238             gl.DepthFunc(GL_LEQUAL);gl.DepthMask(GL_FALSE); 239             gl.DrawElements(GL_LINES,range.outlineCount,GL_UNSIGNED_INT, 240                 reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.outlineFirstIndex*sizeof(GLuint))); 241             gl.DepthFunc(GL_LESS);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE); 242         } 243     } 244     gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,0);gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0);gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.UseProgram(0);return detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster, "Renderer::draw outlines and cleanup"); 245 } 246 inline bool Renderer::renderPass(const Scene& scene,const RenderPass& pass,int width,int height) { 247     Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderPass"); 248     const auto& camera=pass.camera;const auto& options=pass.options;const auto& viewport=pass.viewport; 249     if(!shaderProgramID_||!vao_)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::renderPass","Renderer is not initialized."); 250     if(width<=0||height<=0)return true; 251     if(viewport.x<0 || viewport.y<0 || viewport.x>width || viewport.y>height || viewport.width<0 || viewport.height<0 252         || viewport.width>width-viewport.x || viewport.height>height-viewport.y) 253         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderPass","Viewport lies outside the framebuffer."); 254     if(viewport.width==0 || viewport.height==0) return true; 255     if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(camera)||!detail::finiteVector(options.lightDirection) 256         ||glm::length(options.lightDirection)<1e-12f||!detail::validColor(options.lightColor) 257         ||!detail::validColor(options.outlineColor) 258         ||!std::isfinite(options.background.r)||!std::isfinite(options.background.g) 259         ||!std::isfinite(options.background.b)||!std::isfinite(options.background.a) 260         ||(options.cull!=CullMode::Inherit && options.cull!=CullMode::None && options.cull!=CullMode::Back && options.cull!=CullMode::Front)) 261         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Renderer::renderPass","Invalid camera, light, color or culling mode.","Check near/far planes, FOV, target/up directions, positive orthographic extent and finite render options."); 262     if(uploadedScene_!=&scene||uploadedRevision_!=scene.getGeometryRevision()||uploadedTextures_!=options.textures) { 263         if(!upload(scene,options.textures))return false; 264         uploadedScene_=&scene;uploadedRevision_=scene.getGeometryRevision();uploadedTextures_=options.textures; 265     } 266     auto& gl=detail::gl();if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer, "before Renderer::render"))return false; 267     gl.Viewport(viewport.x,viewport.y,viewport.width,viewport.height);gl.LineWidth(1); 268     if(options.depthTest)gl.Enable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);else gl.Disable(GL_DEPTH_TEST); 269     gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.Disable(GL_BLEND);gl.Enable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST); 270     gl.Scissor(viewport.x,viewport.y,viewport.width,viewport.height); 271     gl.PolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK,GL_FILL);gl.DepthFunc(GL_LESS);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE); 272     gl.ClearColor(options.background.r,options.background.g,options.background.b,options.background.a); 273     const GLbitfield mask=(pass.clearColor?GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT:0)|(pass.clearDepth?GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT:0); 274     if(mask) gl.Clear(mask); 275     if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"viewport/scissor/clear")) {gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);return false;} 276     const bool success=draw(scene,CameraSystem::viewMatrix(camera),CameraSystem::projectionMatrix(camera,float(viewport.width)/viewport.height),options); 277     gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.FrontFace(GL_CCW); 278     return success; 279 } 280 inline bool Renderer::render(const Scene& scene,const Camera& camera,int width,int height,const RenderOptions& options) { 281     RenderPass pass;pass.camera=camera;pass.options=options;pass.viewport={0,0,width,height}; 282     return renderPass(scene,pass,width,height); 283 } 284 inline bool Renderer::renderViews(const Scene& scene,const std::vector<RenderPass>& passes,int width,int height) { 285     if(width<=0 || height<=0) return true; 286     if(passes.empty()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderViews","Need at least one render pass."); 287     if(!shaderProgramID_ || !vao_) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::renderViews","Renderer is not initialized."); 288     auto& gl=detail::gl(); 289     gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);gl.Viewport(0,0,width,height);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE); 290     gl.ClearColor(.08f,.08f,.1f,1);gl.Clear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT|GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT); 291     if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderViews full-frame clear")) return false; 292     for(const auto& pass:passes) if(!renderPass(scene,pass,width,height)) return false; 293     return true; 294 } 295 inline void Renderer::destroy() { 296     auto& gl=detail::gl(); 297     if(ebo_)gl.DeleteBuffers(1,&ebo_);if(vbo_)gl.DeleteBuffers(1,&vbo_);if(vao_)gl.DeleteVertexArrays(1,&vao_); 298     if(shaderProgramID_)gl.DeleteProgram(shaderProgramID_); 299     for(const auto& item:textures_)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second); 300     textures_.clear();drawRanges_.clear();ebo_=vbo_=vao_=shaderProgramID_=0; 301     uploadedScene_=nullptr;uploadedRevision_=std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max();uploadedTextures_=false; 302 } 303 } // namespace graphics

 20     return ++revision;  21 }  22   23 inline void appendFace(Polyhedron& polyhedron, GLFace shape,  24                 std::initializer_list<glm::vec3> vertices, GlColor color) {  25     PlaneFigure face;  26     face.shape = shape;  27     face.localVertices = vertices;  28     face.color = color;  29     face.normal = glm::normalize(glm::cross(  30         face.localVertices[1] - face.localVertices[0],  31         face.localVertices[2] - face.localVertices[0]));  80     }  81     glm::vec3 minimum = polyhedron.faces[0].localVertices[0];  82     glm::vec3 maximum = minimum;  83     for (const PlaneFigure& face : polyhedron.faces) {  84         for (const glm::vec3& vertex : face.localVertices) {  85             minimum = glm::min(minimum, vertex);  86             maximum = glm::max(maximum, vertex);  87         }  88     }  89     if (!finiteVector(maximum - minimum)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::push_entity", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(maximum - minimum)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 207 } 208  209 inline bool Scene::setParent(EntityId id, std::optional<EntityId> parent) { 210     if (id >= components_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setParent", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 222     std::vector<EntityId> chain; 223     std::optional<EntityId> current = id; 224     while (current) { 225         if (*current >= components_.size() || chain.size() >= components_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::worldMatrix", "Rejected condition: *current >= components_.size() || chain.size() >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt); 226         chain.push_back(*current); 227         current = components_[*current].parent; 228     } 229     std::reverse(chain.begin(), chain.end()); 230     LayerId layer = MotionSystem::rootLayer(); 231     std::size_t anchor = chain.size() - 1; 232     for (std::size_t i = chain.size(); i > 0; --i) { 233         const auto binding = getEntityLayer(chain[i - 1]); 234         if (binding) { layer = *binding; 246 } 247 inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::faceWorldMatrix(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex) const { 248     if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::faceWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt); 325 } 326  327 inline std::optional<std::array<LineId, 3>> Scene::addBasis( 328     const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::mat3& basisVectors, 329     const glm::vec3& axisLengths, std::optional<EntityId> owner 330 ) { 331     if (!finiteVector(origin) 332         || !positiveLengths(axisLengths) 333         || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)) { 334         return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(origin) || !positiveLengths(axisLengths) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 335     } 336     for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) { 337         if (!finiteVector(basisVectors[i]) 338             || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f 339             || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])) { 340             return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(basisVectors[i]) || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 341         } 342     } 343     // 사전 검증 후 세 선을 함께 등록한다. 실패 입력은 장면을 변경하지 않는다. 344     const std::array<GlColor, 3> colors{ 345         GlColor{ 1.0f, 0.2f, 0.2f }, 346         GlColor{ 0.2f, 1.0f, 0.2f }, 347         GlColor{ 0.2f, 0.4f, 1.0f } 348     }; 349     std::array<LineId, 3> ids{}; 350     for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) { 351         ids[i] = lines_.size(); 352         lines_.push_back({ 353             origin, origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i], colors[i], owner }); 354     } 355     geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision(); 356     return ids; 357 } 358  359 inline bool Scene::setVector(LineId id, const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::vec3& vector) { 360     if (id >= lines_.size() || !finiteVector(origin) 361         || !finiteVector(origin + vector)) { 362         return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVector", "Rejected condition: id >= lines_.size() || !finiteVector(origin) || !finiteVector(origin + vector)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 363     } 364     lines_[id].start = origin; 365     lines_[id].end = origin + vector; 366     geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision(); 367     return true; 368 } 369  370 inline void Scene::clear() { 371     motions_.clear(); 372     entityLayers_.clear(); 373     if (components_.empty() && lines_.empty()) { 374         return; 375     } 376     polyId_.clear(); 377     components_.clear(); 378     cubeFaceId_.clear(); 379     tetraFaceId_.clear(); 380     pyramidFaceId_.clear(); 381     lines_.clear(); 382     geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision(); 383 } 384  385 inline bool DiagramSystem::validTransform(const Transform3D& transform) { 386     return finiteVector(transform.basis) && finiteVector(transform.localBasis) 387         && finiteVector(transform.scale) 388         && finiteVector(transform.rotation[0]) && finiteVector(transform.rotation[1]) 389         && finiteVector(transform.rotation[2]) 390         && std::abs(glm::determinant(transform.rotation) - 1.0f) < 0.01f 391         && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[0] - glm::vec3(1,0,0)) < 0.01f 392         && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[1] - glm::vec3(0,1,0)) < 0.01f 393         && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[2] - glm::vec3(0,0,1)) < 0.01f 394         && finiteMatrix(detail::transformMatrix(transform)); 395 } 396  397 inline bool DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(const Polyhedron& polyhedron) { 398     if (polyhedron.faces.empty()) return false; 399     for (const auto& face : polyhedron.faces) 400         if (!detail::validMesh(face) || !validTransform(face.transform)) return false; 401     return true; 402 } 403  404 inline Polyhedron DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape shape, const glm::vec3& sideLengths) { 405     Polyhedron polyhedron; 406     if (!positiveLengths(sideLengths)) { 407         return polyhedron; 408     } 409     polyhedron.shape = shape; 410     if (shape == GLShape::Cube) { 411         const float x = sideLengths.x * 0.5f; 412         const float y = sideLengths.y * 0.5f; 413         const float z = sideLengths.z * 0.5f; 414         polyhedron.faces.reserve(6); 415         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect, 416             { { -x, -y, z }, { x, -y, z }, { x, y, z }, { -x, y, z } }, 417             { .9f, .3f, .3f }); 418         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect, 419             { { x, -y, -z }, { -x, -y, -z }, { -x, y, -z }, { x, y, -z } }, 420             { .3f, .8f, .4f }); 421         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect, 422             { { -x, -y, -z }, { -x, -y, z }, { -x, y, z }, { -x, y, -z } }, 423             { .3f, .5f, .9f }); 424         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect, 425             { { x, -y, z }, { x, -y, -z }, { x, y, -z }, { x, y, z } }, 426             { .9f, .8f, .3f }); 427         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect, 428             { { -x, y, z }, { x, y, z }, { x, y, -z }, { -x, y, -z } }, 429             { .3f, .8f, .9f }); 430         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect, 431             { { -x, -y, -z }, { x, -y, -z }, { x, -y, z }, { -x, -y, z } }, 432             { .8f, .3f, .9f }); 433     } else if (shape == GLShape::Pyramid) { 434         const float x = sideLengths.x * 0.5f; 435         const float y = sideLengths.y * 0.5f; 436         const float z = sideLengths.z * 0.5f; 437         const glm::vec3 frontLeft{ -x, -y, z }; 438         const glm::vec3 frontRight{ x, -y, z }; 439         const glm::vec3 backRight{ x, -y, -z }; 440         const glm::vec3 backLeft{ -x, -y, -z }; 441         const glm::vec3 apex{ 0.0f, y, 0.0f }; 442         polyhedron.faces.reserve(5); 443         // 앞, 오른쪽, 뒤, 왼쪽, 바닥. 삼각형과 사각형은 바깥쪽 winding이다. 444         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 445             { frontLeft, frontRight, apex }, { .9f, .3f, .3f }); 446         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 447             { frontRight, backRight, apex }, { .3f, .5f, .9f }); 448         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 449             { backRight, backLeft, apex }, { .3f, .8f, .4f }); 450         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 451             { backLeft, frontLeft, apex }, { .9f, .8f, .3f }); 452         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect, 453             { backLeft, backRight, frontRight, frontLeft }, { .8f, .3f, .9f }); 454     } else if (shape == GLShape::Tetra) { 455         // 원점에서 직교하는 세 모서리를 만들고, 무게중심을 로컬 원점으로 옮긴다. 456         const glm::vec3 center = sideLengths * 0.25f; 457         const glm::vec3 origin = -center; 458         const glm::vec3 x = glm::vec3(sideLengths.x, 0.0f, 0.0f) - center; 459         const glm::vec3 y = glm::vec3(0.0f, sideLengths.y, 0.0f) - center; 460         const glm::vec3 z = glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, sideLengths.z) - center; 461         polyhedron.faces.reserve(4); 462         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 463             { origin, y, x }, { .9f, .4f, .3f }); 464         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 465             { origin, x, z }, { .3f, .8f, .4f }); 466         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 467             { origin, z, y }, { .3f, .5f, .9f }); 468         appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle, 469             { x, y, z }, { .9f, .8f, .3f }); 470     } 471     return polyhedron; 472 } 473  474 inline Model DiagramSystem::makeSphere(float radius,unsigned slices,unsigned stacks) { 475     Model model; 476     if(!std::isfinite(radius)||radius<=0||slices<3||slices>512||stacks<2||stacks>256)return model; 477     Mesh mesh;mesh.localVertices.push_back({0,radius,0}); 478     constexpr double pi=3.14159265358979323846; 479     for(unsigned row=1;row<stacks;++row) { 480         const double latitude=pi*row/stacks; 481         for(unsigned col=0;col<slices;++col) { 482             const double longitude=2*pi*col/slices; 483             mesh.localVertices.push_back(radius*glm::vec3(std::sin(latitude)*std::cos(longitude), 484                 std::cos(latitude),std::sin(latitude)*std::sin(longitude))); 485         } 486     } 487     const auto bottom=std::uint32_t(mesh.localVertices.size()); 488     mesh.localVertices.push_back({0,-radius,0}); 489     for(unsigned col=0;col<slices;++col) { 490         const auto next=(col+1)%slices; 491         mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{0,1+next,1+col}); 492         for(unsigned row=0;row+2<stacks;++row) { 493             const auto a=1+row*slices+col,b=1+row*slices+next,c=a+slices,d=b+slices; 494             mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{a,b,c,b,d,c}); 495         } 496         const auto a=1+(stacks-2)*slices+col,b=1+(stacks-2)*slices+next; 497         mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{a,b,bottom}); 498     } 499     for(const auto& p:mesh.localVertices)mesh.vertexNormals.push_back(glm::normalize(p)); 500     model.faces.push_back(std::move(mesh)); 501     return validPolyhedron(model)?model:Model{}; 502 } 503 inline Model DiagramSystem::makeCone(float radius,float height,unsigned slices) { 504     Model model; 505     if(!std::isfinite(radius)||!std::isfinite(height)||radius<=0||height<=0||slices<3||slices>512)return model; 506     constexpr double pi=3.14159265358979323846; 507     // Separate sides/base keep the bottom rim sharp for normals and outlines. 508     Mesh sides,base;sides.localVertices.push_back({0,height*.5f,0}); 509     base.localVertices.push_back({0,-height*.5f,0}); 510     for(unsigned i=0;i<slices;++i) { 511         const double a=2*pi*i/slices; 512         const glm::vec3 p{radius*float(std::cos(a)),-height*.5f,radius*float(std::sin(a))}; 513         sides.localVertices.push_back(p);base.localVertices.push_back(p); 514     } 515     for(unsigned i=0;i<slices;++i) { 516         const auto a=1+i,b=1+(i+1)%slices; 517         sides.triangleIndices.insert(sides.triangleIndices.end(),{0,b,a}); 518         base.triangleIndices.insert(base.triangleIndices.end(),{0,a,b}); 519     } 520     recalculateNormals(sides);recalculateNormals(base); 521     model.faces={std::move(sides),std::move(base)}; 522     return validPolyhedron(model)?model:Model{}; 523 } 524  525 inline glm::mat4 DiagramSystem::modelMatrix(const Transform3D& transform) { 526     return detail::transformMatrix(transform); 527 } 528  529 inline glm::mat4 DiagramSystem::faceModelMatrix(const Component_Poly& component, 530                                          const PlaneFigure& face) { 531     return detail::faceMatrix(component, face); 532 } 533  534 inline bool CameraSystem::validCamera(const Camera& camera) { 535     const glm::vec3 direction = camera.target - camera.position; 536     const glm::vec3 right = glm::cross(direction, camera.up); 537     return finiteVector(camera.position) && finiteVector(camera.target) 538         && finiteVector(camera.up) && finiteVector(direction) && finiteVector(right) 539         && std::isfinite(glm::dot(direction, direction)) 540         && std::isfinite(glm::dot(right, right)) 541         && glm::dot(direction, direction) > 0.000001f 542         && glm::dot(right, right) > 0.000001f 543         && std::isfinite(camera.fieldOfViewDegrees) 544         && camera.fieldOfViewDegrees > 0.0f && camera.fieldOfViewDegrees < 180.0f 545         && std::isfinite(camera.nearPlane) && std::isfinite(camera.farPlane) 546         && camera.nearPlane > 0.0f && camera.farPlane > camera.nearPlane 547         && std::isfinite(camera.orthographicHalfExtent) 548         && camera.orthographicHalfExtent > 0.0f; 549 } 550  551 inline glm::mat4 CameraSystem::viewMatrix(const Camera& camera) { 552     return glm::lookAt(camera.position, camera.target, camera.up); 553 } 554  555 inline glm::mat4 CameraSystem::projectionMatrix(const Camera& camera, float aspect) { 556     if (camera.orthographic) { 557         const float halfWidth = camera.orthographicHalfExtent * (aspect >= 1.0f ? aspect : 1.0f); 558         const float halfHeight = camera.orthographicHalfExtent / (aspect < 1.0f ? aspect : 1.0f); 559         return glm::ortho(-halfWidth, halfWidth, -halfHeight, halfHeight, 560                           camera.nearPlane, camera.farPlane); 561     } 562     return glm::perspective(glm::radians(camera.fieldOfViewDegrees), aspect, 563                             camera.nearPlane, camera.farPlane); 564 } 565  566  567 inline void Scene::rebuildFaceIds() { 568     cubeFaceId_.clear(); tetraFaceId_.clear(); pyramidFaceId_.clear(); 569     for (EntityId id : polyId_) { 570         const auto& model = components_[id].polyhedron; 571         auto* ids = model.shape == GLShape::Cube ? &cubeFaceId_ 572             : model.shape == GLShape::Tetra ? &tetraFaceId_ 573             : model.shape == GLShape::Pyramid ? &pyramidFaceId_ : nullptr; 574         if(ids) for(std::size_t f=0; f<model.faces.size(); ++f) ids->push_back({id,f}); 575     } 576 } 577 inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addModel(const Model& model, const Transform3D& transform, 578                                              PivotMode pivot, const glm::vec3& customPivot) { 579     if(!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model) || !detail::finiteVector(customPivot)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addModel", "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model) || !detail::finiteVector(customPivot)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt); 580     auto initial=transform; 581     if(pivot==PivotMode::BottomCenter) initial.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(model); 582     else if(pivot==PivotMode::Custom) initial.localBasis=customPivot; 583     auto id=push_entity(model,initial); 584     if(id) components_[*id].pivotMode=pivot; 585     return id; 586 } 587 inline bool Scene::setModel(EntityId id, const Model& model) { 588     if(id>=components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setModel", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 589     const auto box=detail::bounds(model); 590     if(!detail::finiteVector(box.second-box.first)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setModel", "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(box.second-box.first)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 591     auto replacement=components_[id]; 592     replacement.polyhedron=model;replacement.sideLengths=box.second-box.first;replacement.transformOnly=false; 593     if(replacement.pivotMode==PivotMode::BottomCenter) 594         replacement.transform.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(model); 595     components_[id]=std::move(replacement); 596     rebuildFaceIds();geometryRevision_=nextGeometryRevision();return true; 597 } 598 inline const Mesh* Scene::getMesh(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex) const { 599     const auto* c=getComponent(id); 600     return c && meshIndex<c->polyhedron.faces.size()?&c->polyhedron.faces[meshIndex]:nullptr; 601 } 602 inline bool Scene::setMesh(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex, const Mesh& mesh) { 603     if(!getMesh(id,meshIndex)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMesh", "Rejected condition: !getMesh(id,meshIndex)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id); 604     Model replacement=components_[id].polyhedron; 605     replacement.faces[meshIndex]=mesh; 606     return setModel(id,replacement); 607 } 608 inline bool Scene::setMeshVertices(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex, 609                                    const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices, 610                                    const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& triangles) { 611     const auto* original=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!original)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMeshVertices", "Rejected condition: !original", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
