   1 #pragma once   2 #include "Application.h"   3 #include "GridLayout.h"   4 #include <random>   5    6 namespace graphics {   7 struct Bounds3 {   8     glm::vec3 minimum{-1}, maximum{1};   9     /// [API] 해당 데이터가 계산에 필요한 유효 범위와 유한성 조건을 만족하는지 반환한다.  10     bool valid() const;  11     /// [API] 점이 margin만큼 안쪽으로 줄인 Bounds3 안에 있는지 검사한다.  12     bool contains(const glm::vec3& point,const glm::vec3& margin=glm::vec3(0)) const;  13     /// [API] 두 축 정렬 경계 상자가 겹치는지 검사한다.  14     bool overlaps(const Bounds3& other) const;  15 };  16 struct PathPose { glm::vec3 position{0}, tangent{0,0,1}; float yawDegrees=0; };  17 class DistancePath {  18     std::vector<glm::vec3> points_;  19     std::vector<double> distances_;  20 public:  21     /// [API] 연속 점들의 누적 거리 경로를 구성한다. closed면 마지막에서 처음으로 연결한다.  22     bool setPoints(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points,bool closed=false);  23     /// [API] 경로 전체 누적 길이를 반환한다. 경로가 비어 있으면 0이다.  24     double length() const { return distances_.empty()?0:distances_.back(); }  25     /// [API] 누적 거리와 Once/Loop/PingPong 정책에서 위치·접선·yaw를 샘플링한다.  26     std::optional<PathPose> sample(double distance,Playback playback=Playback::Loop) const;  27 };  28 struct AnimationClock {  29     double seconds=0;  30     bool playing=true;  31     /// [API] playing일 때만 누적 시간을 늘린다. dt와 누적 값의 유한성·범위를 검사한다.  32     bool advance(double dt);  33     /// [API] 누적 시간을 0으로 되돌리고 재생 상태를 켠다.  34     void reset() { seconds=0;playing=true; }  35 };  36 struct RoomBody {  37     glm::vec3 localPosition{0}, localVelocity{0}, halfExtent{.2f};  38     glm::vec3 worldPosition{0}, worldVelocity{0};  39     bool released=false;  40     /// [API] 이탈 전에는 roomWorld로 로컬 중심을 월드로 변환하고 이탈 후에는 worldPosition을 반환한다.  41     glm::vec3 position(const glm::mat4& roomWorld) const {  42         return released?worldPosition:glm::vec3(roomWorld*glm::vec4(localPosition,1));  43     }  44 };  45 struct CharacterBody {  46     glm::vec3 feet{0}, direction{0,0,1};  47     glm::vec2 halfWidth{.25f};  48     float height=1.6f, speed=1, verticalSpeed=0;  49     double walkDistance=0;  50     bool grounded=true;  51 };  52 struct CharacterStep { bool blocked=false, turned=false, landed=false; };  53 class CollisionSystem {  54 public:  55     // Rigid, kinematic room. Boxes stay aligned to its axes until release.  56     /// [API] 방 내부에서는 로컬 반사/중력을 계산하고 released 뒤에는 월드 중력으로 적분한다.  57     static bool stepRoomBody(RoomBody& body,const Bounds3& room,const glm::mat4& roomWorld,  58                              float dt,bool floorOpen,bool elastic,  59                              const glm::vec3& gravity=glm::vec3(0,-9.8f,0),  60                              const glm::vec3& angularVelocity=glm::vec3(0));  61     /// [API] 접지 상태에서만 위쪽 속도를 설정한다. 공중 입력 거절은 정상적인 false다.  62     static bool jump(CharacterBody& body,float speed);  63     /// [API] 수평 이동·장애물/경계 반전·중력·윗면 착지·지지면 이탈을 하위 단계로 계산한다.  64     static std::optional<CharacterStep> stepCharacter(CharacterBody& body,const Bounds3& stage,  65                              const std::vector<Bounds3>& obstacles,float dt,float gravity=9.8f);  66     /// [API] 제한 횟수 안에서 occupied와 겹치지 않는 배치를 찾는다. 난수 엔진의 시드·수명은 호출자가 관리한다.  67     static std::optional<Bounds3> randomFreeBox(const Bounds3& area,const glm::vec3& size,  68                              const std::vector<Bounds3>& occupied,std::mt19937& random,unsigned attempts=128);  69 };  70 struct JointRig {  71     EntityId root=0, head=0;  72     std::array<EntityId,2> shoulders{}, hips{};  73 };  74 class PatternSystem {  75 public:  76     /// [API] 직선 → 안쪽 코너 중심 원호 → 직선의 거리에서 위치·접선·yaw를 계산한다. 끝에서 처음으로 순환한다.  77     static std::optional<PathPose> conveyorCornerPose(double distance,const glm::vec3& start,const glm::vec3& pivot,float radius,float endZ);  78     /// [API] 양수 크기의 중심 기준 모델을 만들고 부모 로컬 위치·색·부모 관계를 설정한다.  79     static std::optional<EntityId> addBox(Scene& scene,const glm::vec3& size,const glm::vec3& position,  80                                          GlColor color,std::optional<EntityId> parent=std::nullopt);  81     /// [API] 점 목록으로 단일 다각형 면을 만들고 색·부모 관계와 함께 Scene에 등록한다. 오목 다각형의 자동 분할을 가정하지 않는다.  82     static std::optional<EntityId> addPolygon(Scene& scene,const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points,  83                                          GlColor color,std::optional<EntityId> parent=std::nullopt);  84     /// [API] 여섯 면의 방을 만들고 바깥 winding에 Front cull, 안쪽 조명 법선을 적용한다. face 0..3은 옆벽, 4 천장, 5 바닥이다.  85     static std::optional<EntityId> addRoom(Scene& scene,const Bounds3& bounds,bool frontOpen=true);  86     /// [API] 부모에 부착된 transform-only 그룹을 만든다. 표시 메시 크기와 회전 연결 위치를 분리한다.  87     static std::optional<EntityId> addJoint(Scene& scene,EntityId parent,const glm::vec3& anchor);  88     /// [API] 연결 위치와 크기를 유지하면서 회전 기저를 변경한다.  89     static bool rotateJoint(Scene& scene,EntityId joint,const glm::vec3& axis,float degrees);  90     /// [API] DistancePath 샘플을 엔티티 위치와 +Z 기준 Y 회전에 반영한다.  91     static bool follow(Scene& scene,EntityId entity,const DistancePath& path,double distance,  92                         const glm::vec3& offset=glm::vec3(0),bool orient=true);  93     /// [API] 0~9를 선분으로 구성한다. 부모 로컬 XY 평면의 표시이며 일반 폰트/텍스트 렌더러는 아니다.  94     static bool addNumber(Scene& scene,unsigned digit,const glm::vec3& position,float height,  95                           GlColor color,std::optional<EntityId> parent=std::nullopt);  96     /// [API] 지정 회전축을 중심으로 면 로컬 애니메이션 행렬을 계산한다.  97     static bool foldFace(Scene& scene,EntityId entity,std::size_t face,const AxisLine& hinge,float degrees);  98     /// [API] 진행률 [0,1]로 이동 또는 상단 피벗 Y축 축소를 적용한다. 완전 개방은 숨기고 0 배율 특이행렬을 피한다.  99     static bool openFace(Scene& scene,EntityId entity,std::size_t face,float progress, 100                          const glm::vec3& displacement,const glm::vec3& topPivot,bool scaleOpen); 101     /// [API] 시선 target-position과 옆벽의 월드 바깥 법선 내적이 가장 큰 양수인 면을 선택한다. 외부 카메라에서 보이는 맞은편 내부 벽이며 수직 시선에는 선택이 없을 수 있다. 102     static std::optional<std::size_t> cameraFacingWall(const Scene& scene,EntityId room,const Camera& camera); 103     /// [API] 선택 옆벽의 정점에서 상단 피벗을 계산하고 openFace로 높이를 축소/복원한다. 104     static bool openRoomWall(Scene& scene,EntityId room,std::size_t face,float progress); 105     /// [API] 두 ID의 위치는 유지하고 모델을 교환한다. 106     static bool swapModels(Scene& scene,EntityId first,EntityId second); 107     /// [API] 셀 격자를 지그재그 순서로 방문하는 정수 인덱스 목록을 만든다. 108     static std::vector<glm::ivec3> snakeCells(const glm::ivec3& cells); 109     /// [API] 두 값의 차이가 허용 오차 이하인지 검사한다. 110     static bool aligned(float first,float second,float tolerance); 111     /// [API] 좌표를 양수 간격으로 반올림한다. 실패 시 입력 점을 그대로 유지한다. 112     static glm::vec3 snap(const glm::vec3& point,float spacing); 113     /// [API] 화면 픽셀을 ray로 바꾸어 평면 교차 위치를 얻는다. 평행/뒤쪽 교차는 빈 결과다. 114     static std::optional<glm::vec3> screenOnPlane(const Camera& camera,double x,double y, 115                          const PickViewport& viewport,const glm::vec3& planePoint,const glm::vec3& normal); 116     /// [API] 시점과 주목점을 동일하게 이동해 시선 방향을 보존한다. 117     static bool moveCamera(Camera& camera,const glm::vec3& offset); 118     /// [API] 시점은 고정하고 카메라 로컬 up축을 기준으로 시선을 회전한다. 119     static bool yawCamera(Camera& camera,float degrees); 120     /// [API] 월드 축에 대해 시점·주목점·up을 함께 변환한다. 축을 target에 두면 target은 고정된다. 121     static bool orbitCamera(Camera& camera,const AxisLine& axis,float degrees); 122     /// [API] 원근은 target까지 거리, 직각은 orthographicHalfExtent를 배율로 바꾼다. 123     static bool zoomCamera(Camera& camera,float factor); 127     /// [API] 그리드 비율로 머리·코·몸통·어깨·엉덩이·팔·다리 계층을 조립하고 ID 묶음을 반환한다. 128     static std::optional<JointRig> addWalkingRig(Scene& scene,const GridLayout& bodyGrid); 129     /// [API] 루트 이동/yaw와 실제 이동 거리 기반 팔다리 반대 위상을 적용한다. 속도는 보행 진폭에 반영한다. 130     static bool poseWalkingRig(Scene& scene,const JointRig& rig,const glm::vec3& feet,float yawDegrees, 131                                double walkDistance,float speed); framework/include/Picking.h
   1 #pragma once   2 #include "Graphics.h"   3    4 namespace graphics {   5 struct Ray {   6     glm::vec3 origin{0}, direction{0,0,-1};   7     float maxDistance = std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity();   8 };   9 struct PickResult {  10     EntityId entityId = 0;  11     std::size_t faceIndex = 0, triangleIndex = 0;  12     glm::vec3 worldPoint{0}, localPoint{0}, barycentric{0};  13     glm::vec2 uv{0};  14     bool hasUV = false;  15     float distance = 0; // Distance from ray origin (near plane for a screen ray).  16 };  17 struct PickViewport { double x = 0, y = 0, width = 0, height = 0; }; // top-left framebuffer pixels  18   19 /// [API] 왼쪽 위 기준 프레임버퍼 픽셀을 카메라 ray로 역투영한다. 영역 밖/잘못된 투영이면 빈 결과다.  20 inline std::optional<Ray> screenRay(const Camera& camera,double x,double y,const PickViewport& viewport) {  21     if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(camera)||!std::isfinite(x)||!std::isfinite(y)  22         ||!std::isfinite(viewport.x)||!std::isfinite(viewport.y)  23         ||!std::isfinite(viewport.width)||!std::isfinite(viewport.height)  24         ||viewport.width<=0||viewport.height<=0||x<viewport.x||y<viewport.y  25         ||x>=viewport.x+viewport.width||y>=viewport.y+viewport.height)return {};  26     const auto pv=CameraSystem::projectionMatrix(camera,float(viewport.width/viewport.height))*CameraSystem::viewMatrix(camera);  27     const auto determinant=glm::determinant(pv);  28     if(!std::isfinite(determinant)||std::abs(determinant)<1e-12f)return {};  29     const auto inverse=glm::inverse(pv);  30     const float nx=float(2*(x-viewport.x)/viewport.width-1),ny=float(1-2*(y-viewport.y)/viewport.height);  31     auto nearPoint=inverse*glm::vec4(nx,ny,-1,1),farPoint=inverse*glm::vec4(nx,ny,1,1);  32     if(std::abs(nearPoint.w)<1e-12f||std::abs(farPoint.w)<1e-12f)return {};  33     nearPoint/=nearPoint.w;farPoint/=farPoint.w;  34     const auto direction=glm::vec3(farPoint-nearPoint);const auto length=glm::length(direction);  35     if(!detail::finiteVector(glm::vec3(nearPoint))||!detail::finiteVector(direction)||!std::isfinite(length)||length<=0)return {};  36     return Ray{glm::vec3(nearPoint),direction/length,length};  37 }  38 /// [API] ray 또는 화면 좌표로 보이는 삼각형의 최근접 교차를 찾아 객체·면·위치·UV를 반환한다.  39 inline std::optional<PickResult> pick(const Scene& scene,const Ray& input) {  40     if(!detail::finiteVector(input.origin)||!detail::finiteVector(input.direction)  41         ||!std::isfinite(glm::length(input.direction))||glm::length(input.direction)<1e-12f||std::isnan(input.maxDistance)||input.maxDistance<0)return {};  42     const auto direction=glm::dvec3(glm::normalize(input.direction));  43     const auto origin=glm::dvec3(input.origin);  44     double nearest=input.maxDistance;std::optional<PickResult> result;  45     for(auto id:scene.getIds()) {  46         const auto* component=scene.getComponent(id);if(!component||!component->visible)continue;  47         for(std::size_t f=0;f<component->polyhedron.faces.size();++f) {  48             const auto& face=component->polyhedron.faces[f];if(!face.visible)continue;  49             const auto world=scene.faceWorldMatrix(id,f);if(!world)continue;  50             const auto matrix=*world;  51             const auto indices=detail::triangles(face);  52             for(std::size_t t=0;t<indices.size();t+=3) {  53                 const auto ia=indices[t],ib=indices[t+1],ic=indices[t+2];  54                 const auto a4=matrix*glm::vec4(face.localVertices[ia],1),b4=matrix*glm::vec4(face.localVertices[ib],1),c4=matrix*glm::vec4(face.localVertices[ic],1);  55                 if(std::abs(a4.w)<1e-12f||std::abs(b4.w)<1e-12f||std::abs(c4.w)<1e-12f)continue;  56                 const auto a=glm::dvec3(a4/a4.w),b=glm::dvec3(b4/b4.w),c=glm::dvec3(c4/c4.w);  57                 const auto edge1=b-a,edge2=c-a,p=glm::cross(direction,edge2);  58                 const auto determinant=glm::dot(edge1,p);  59                 const auto tolerance=1e-12*glm::length(edge1)*glm::length(edge2);  60                 if(!std::isfinite(determinant)||std::abs(determinant)<=tolerance)continue;  61                 const auto relative=origin-a;const auto u=glm::dot(relative,p)/determinant;  62                 if(u<0||u>1)continue;  63                 const auto q=glm::cross(relative,edge1);const auto v=glm::dot(direction,q)/determinant;  64                 if(v<0||u+v>1)continue;  65                 const auto distance=glm::dot(edge2,q)/determinant;  66                 if(!std::isfinite(distance)||distance<0||distance>nearest)continue;  67                 const glm::dvec3 weights((1-u-v)/a4.w,u/b4.w,v/c4.w);  68                 const auto sum=weights.x+weights.y+weights.z;if(std::abs(sum)<1e-12)continue;  69                 PickResult hit;hit.entityId=id;hit.faceIndex=f;hit.triangleIndex=t/3;  70                 hit.distance=float(distance);hit.worldPoint=glm::vec3(origin+direction*distance);  71                 hit.barycentric=glm::vec3(weights/sum);  72                 hit.localPoint=face.localVertices[ia]*hit.barycentric.x+face.localVertices[ib]*hit.barycentric.y+face.localVertices[ic]*hit.barycentric.z;  73                 hit.hasUV=!face.texcoords.empty();  74                 if(hit.hasUV)hit.uv=face.texcoords[ia]*hit.barycentric.x+face.texcoords[ib]*hit.barycentric.y+face.texcoords[ic]*hit.barycentric.z;  75                 nearest=distance;result=hit;  76             }  77         }  78     }  79     return result;  80 }  81 /// [API] ray 또는 화면 좌표로 보이는 삼각형의 최근접 교차를 찾아 객체·면·위치·UV를 반환한다.  82 inline std::optional<PickResult> pick(const Scene& scene,const Camera& camera,double x,double y,  83                                      const PickViewport& viewport) {  84     const auto ray=screenRay(camera,x,y,viewport);return ray?pick(scene,*ray):std::nullopt;  85 }  86 } // namespace graphics
framework/include/Texture.h
   1 #pragma once   2 #include "Model.h"   3 #include "../detail/Native.h"   4 #include <wincodec.h>   5 #include <stdexcept>   6 #include <algorithm>   7 #pragma comment(lib, "ole32.lib")   8 #pragma comment(lib, "windowscodecs.lib")   9   10 namespace graphics {  11 namespace detail {  12 template<class T> struct ComOwner {  13     T* value = nullptr;  14     ~ComOwner() { if(value) value->Release(); }  15     ComOwner() = default;  16     ComOwner(const ComOwner&) = delete;  17     ComOwner& operator=(const ComOwner&) = delete;  18 };  19 inline void checkCom(HRESULT result) {  20     if(FAILED(result)) throw std::runtime_error("Image decoder failed (HRESULT "+std::to_string(result)+").");  21 }  22 struct ComApartment {  23     HRESULT result = CoInitializeEx(nullptr,COINIT_MULTITHREADED);  24     ComApartment() { if(result!=RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE) checkCom(result); }  25     ~ComApartment() { if(SUCCEEDED(result)) CoUninitialize(); }  26 };  27 inline TextureImage decodeImage(const std::filesystem::path& file,  28                                const std::vector<std::uint8_t>* encoded,  29                                bool flipRows) {  30     ComApartment apartment;  31     ComOwner<IWICImagingFactory> factory;  32     checkCom(CoCreateInstance(CLSID_WICImagingFactory,nullptr,CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER,  33                              IID_PPV_ARGS(&factory.value)));  34     ComOwner<IWICStream> stream;  35     ComOwner<IWICBitmapDecoder> decoder;  36     if(encoded) {  37         if(encoded->empty() || encoded->size()>std::numeric_limits<DWORD>::max())  38             throw std::runtime_error("Invalid encoded texture size.");  39         checkCom(factory.value->CreateStream(&stream.value));  40         checkCom(stream.value->InitializeFromMemory(const_cast<BYTE*>(encoded->data()),DWORD(encoded->size())));  41         checkCom(factory.value->CreateDecoderFromStream(stream.value,nullptr,WICDecodeMetadataCacheOnLoad,&decoder.value));  42     } else checkCom(factory.value->CreateDecoderFromFilename(file.c_str(),nullptr,GENERIC_READ,  43                                                             WICDecodeMetadataCacheOnLoad,&decoder.value));  44     ComOwner<IWICBitmapFrameDecode> frame;  45     ComOwner<IWICFormatConverter> converter;  46     checkCom(decoder.value->GetFrame(0,&frame.value));  47     checkCom(factory.value->CreateFormatConverter(&converter.value));  48     checkCom(converter.value->Initialize(frame.value,GUID_WICPixelFormat32bppRGBA,WICBitmapDitherTypeNone,  49                                         nullptr,0,WICBitmapPaletteTypeCustom));  50     TextureImage image;  51     checkCom(converter.value->GetSize(&image.width,&image.height));  52     if(!image.width || !image.height || image.width>16384 || image.height>16384)  53         throw std::runtime_error("Texture dimensions must be within 1..16384.");  54     image.rgba.resize(std::size_t(image.width)*image.height*4);  55     checkCom(converter.value->CopyPixels(nullptr,image.width*4,UINT(image.rgba.size()),image.rgba.data()));  56     if(flipRows) {  57         const std::size_t stride=std::size_t(image.width)*4;  58         for(std::uint32_t y=0;y<image.height/2;++y)  59             std::swap_ranges(image.rgba.begin()+y*stride,image.rgba.begin()+(y+1)*stride,  60                              image.rgba.begin()+(image.height-1-y)*stride);  61     }  62     return image;  63 }  64 } // namespace detail  65 } // namespace graphics
framework/include/Transform.h
   1 #pragma once   2 #include "Model.h"   3 #include <functional>   4    5 namespace graphics {   6    7 struct AxisLine {   8     glm::vec3 point{0.0f};   9     glm::vec3 direction{0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f};  10 };  11   12 using PathFunction = std::function<glm::vec3(double)>;  13 using InterpolationFunction = std::function<double(double)>;  14   15 // These functions return no matrix/path when their parameters are invalid.  16 class TransformSystem {  17 public:  18     /// [API] 행렬 원소가 유한하고 마지막 행이 아핀 변환 형식인지 검사한다.  19     static bool validAffine(const glm::mat4& matrix);  20     /// [API] 이동 벡터로 아핀 이동 행렬을 만든다. 유효하지 않으면 빈 optional이다.  21     static std::optional<glm::mat4> translation(const glm::vec3& offset);  22     /// [API] 중심점 주위의 축별 배율 행렬 T(C)·S·T(-C)를 만든다.  23     static std::optional<glm::mat4> scaling(const glm::vec3& factors,  24                                            const glm::vec3& center = glm::vec3(0));  25     /// [API] 직선 위 점·축 방향과 도 단위 각도로 축 주위 회전 행렬을 만든다.  26     static std::optional<glm::mat4> rotation(const AxisLine& axis, double degrees);  27     /// [API] 주어진 점을 중심으로 세 축을 반전하는 점 반사 행렬을 만든다.  28     static std::optional<glm::mat4> reflectPoint(const glm::vec3& center);  29     /// [API] 주어진 직선을 기준으로 선 반사 행렬을 만든다.  30     static std::optional<glm::mat4> reflectLine(const AxisLine& line);  31     /// [API] 평면의 점과 법선으로 평면 반사 행렬을 만든다.  32     static std::optional<glm::mat4> reflectPlane(const glm::vec3& point,  33                                                const glm::vec3& normal);  34 };  35   36 class PathSystem {  37 public:  38     // axisU/axisV include the ellipse radii and define its plane.  39     /// [API] 중심과 두 반축 벡터로 매개변수 타원 경로 함수를 만든다.  40     static PathFunction ellipse(const glm::vec3& center, const glm::vec3& axisU,  41                                 const glm::vec3& axisV);  42     /// [API] 중심·반지름·평면 법선으로 원 경로 함수를 만든다.  43     static PathFunction circle(const glm::vec3& center, float radius,  44                                const glm::vec3& normal = glm::vec3(0, 1, 0));  45     /// [API] 점 목록을 따라가는 꺾은선 경로 함수를 만든다. closed로 순환 여부를 정한다.  46     static PathFunction polyline(std::vector<glm::vec3> points, bool closed = false);  47     /// [API] 마지막 점과 첫 점이 연결되는 닫힌 꺾은선 경로 함수를 만든다.  48     static PathFunction polygon(std::vector<glm::vec3> points);  49     /// [API] 중심과 두 반변 벡터로 직사각형 테두리 경로 함수를 만든다.  50     static PathFunction rectangle(const glm::vec3& center, const glm::vec3& halfU,  51                                   const glm::vec3& halfV);  52     /// [API] 입력 진행률을 선형 보간 진행률로 반환한다.  53     static double linear(double progress);  54     /// [API] 진행률에 3t²-2t³ 완화 곡선을 적용한다.  55     static double smoothStep(double progress);  56 };  57 } // namespace graphics  58 #include "../detail/Transform.inl"
openGL_3dAssign_20/Console.cpp
   1 #include "Console.h"   2 #include <algorithm>   3 #include <iostream>   4 #include <stdexcept>   5    6 namespace {   7 constexpr float beltHeight = 1.0f;   8 constexpr float boxSize = .85f;   9 constexpr double beltSpeed = 1.25;  10 constexpr std::size_t stripeCount = 30;  11 constexpr float cornerRadius = .8f;  12 constexpr double halfPi = 1.57079632679489661923;  13 const glm::vec3 conveyorStart{-4, beltHeight, -2};  14 const glm::vec3 cornerPivot{2.2f, beltHeight, -1.2f};  15 constexpr float conveyorEndZ = 3.0f;  16 const double conveyorLength = (double(cornerPivot.x) - conveyorStart.x)  17     + halfPi * double(cornerRadius) + (double(conveyorEndZ) - cornerPivot.z);  18   19 void require(bool success, const char* operation) {  20     if (!success) throw std::runtime_error(operation);  21 }  22 }  23   24 bool Console::initScene() {  25     auto& scene = app_.getScene();  26     scene.clear();  27     stripes_.clear();  28   29     require(graphics::PatternSystem::addPolygon(scene,  30         {{-6, 0, -4}, {-6, 0, 5}, {6, 0, 5}, {6, 0, -4}},  31         {.25f, .30f, .34f}).has_value(), "Floor creation failed.");  32     // Two convex planes meet at an edge: an L is not a single convex polygon.  33     require(graphics::PatternSystem::addPolygon(scene,  34         {{-4.8f, beltHeight, -2.8f}, {-4.8f, beltHeight, -1.2f},  35          {3.8f, beltHeight, -1.2f}, {3.8f, beltHeight, -2.8f}},  36         {.09f, .12f, .15f}).has_value(), "Horizontal conveyor plane creation failed.");  37     require(graphics::PatternSystem::addPolygon(scene,  38         {{2.2f, beltHeight, -1.2f}, {2.2f, beltHeight, 3.8f},  39          {3.8f, beltHeight, 3.8f}, {3.8f, beltHeight, -1.2f}},  40         {.09f, .12f, .15f}).has_value(), "Vertical conveyor plane creation failed.");  41   42     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < stripeCount; ++i) {  43         const auto stripe = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {1.4f, .03f, .10f},  44             {0, 0, 0}, {.72f, .77f, .80f});  45         require(stripe.has_value(), "Conveyor stripe creation failed.");  46         stripes_.push_back(*stripe);  47     }  48   49     const std::array<graphics::GlColor, 5> colors{{  50         {.95f, .30f, .23f}, {.23f, .72f, .96f}, {.98f, .75f, .20f},  51         {.38f, .86f, .43f}, {.78f, .44f, .96f}}};  52     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < boxes_.size(); ++i) {  53         const auto box = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene,  54             {boxSize, boxSize, boxSize}, {0, 0, 0}, colors[i]);  55         require(box.has_value(), "Parcel creation failed.");  56         boxes_[i] = *box;  57         // Each outward face has its own label frame, so camera orbit keeps digits readable.  58         for (int face = 0; face < 4; ++face) {  59             const auto label = graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene, *box, {0, 0, 0});  60             require(label.has_value(), "Parcel label frame creation failed.");  61             require(graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene, *label, {0, 1, 0},  62                 float(face) * 90.0f), "Parcel label frame rotation failed.");  63             require(graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene, unsigned(i + 1),  64                 {-.12f, -.24f, boxSize * .5f + .008f}, .48f,  65                 {.04f, .04f, .06f}, *label), "Parcel number creation failed.");  66         }  67     }  68   69     auto& options = app_.getRenderOptions();  70     options = graphics::RenderOptions{};  71     options.depthTest = true;  72     options.lighting = true;  73     options.background = {.08f, .10f, .14f, 1};  74     resetState();  75     std::cout << "실습 20: 택배 물류 센터\n"  76         << "b 이동 시작/정지 | +/- 확대/축소 | p 원근 / P 직각\n"  77         << "좌/우 중심 Y축 공전 | r 전체 리셋 | q 종료\n";  78     return true;  79 }  80   81 void Console::placeMovingObjects() {  82     auto& scene = app_.getScene();  83     const double travelled = clock_.seconds * beltSpeed;  84     const auto place = [&](graphics::EntityId entity, double distance, float height) {  85         const auto pose = graphics::PatternSystem::conveyorCornerPose(distance,  86             conveyorStart, cornerPivot, cornerRadius, conveyorEndZ);  87         require(pose.has_value(), "Conveyor corner pose sampling failed.");  88         const auto* component = scene.getComponent(entity);  89         require(component != nullptr, "Conveyor moving entity is missing.");  90         auto transform = component->transform;  91         transform.basis = pose->position + glm::vec3(0, height, 0);  92         transform.rotation = glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),  93             glm::radians(pose->yawDegrees), glm::vec3(0, 1, 0)));  94         require(scene.setTransform(entity, transform), "Conveyor moving entity transform failed.");  95     };  96     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < stripes_.size(); ++i) {  97         const double offset = conveyorLength * double(i) / double(stripes_.size());  98         place(stripes_[i], travelled + offset, .015f);  99     } 100     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < boxes_.size(); ++i) { 101         const double offset = conveyorLength * double(i) / double(boxes_.size()); 102         place(boxes_[i], travelled + offset, .03f + boxSize * .5f); 103     } 104 } 105  106 void Console::resetState() { 107     clock_.reset(); 108     clock_.playing = false; 109     leftHeld_ = false; 110     rightHeld_ = false; 111     auto& camera = app_.getCamera(); 112     camera = graphics::Camera{}; 113     camera.position = {11, 10, 13}; 114     camera.target = {0, .7f, .3f}; 115     camera.up = {0, 1, 0}; 116     camera.orthographic = false; 117     camera.orthographicHalfExtent = 7.5f; 118     camera.nearPlane = .1f; 119     camera.farPlane = 120; 120     require(graphics::CameraSystem::validCamera(camera), "Reset camera is invalid."); 121     placeMovingObjects(); 122 } 123  124 void Console::zoom(float factor) { 125     auto& camera = app_.getCamera(); 126     const float current = camera.orthographic ? camera.orthographicHalfExtent 127         : glm::length(camera.position - camera.target); 128     // Keep useful scene visibility and stay within the far clipping distance. 129     const float next = std::clamp(current * factor, 2.0f, camera.orthographic ? 35.0f : 60.0f); 130     require(graphics::PatternSystem::zoomCamera(camera, next / current), "Camera zoom failed."); 131 } 132  133 void Console::onKey(int key, int action, int mods) { 134     if (key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT) leftHeld_ = action != GLFW_RELEASE; 135     if (key == GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) rightHeld_ = action != GLFW_RELEASE; 136     if (action != GLFW_PRESS && action != GLFW_REPEAT) return; 137     if (key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD) zoom(.9f); 138     if (key == GLFW_KEY_MINUS || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) zoom(1.0f / .9f); 139     if (action != GLFW_PRESS) return; 140     switch (key) { 141     case GLFW_KEY_B: 142         clock_.playing = !clock_.playing; 143         break; 144     case GLFW_KEY_P: 145         // Shift and Caps Lock together produce lowercase p. 146         app_.getCamera().orthographic = ((mods & GLFW_MOD_SHIFT) != 0) 147             != ((mods & GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK) != 0); 148         break; 149     case GLFW_KEY_R: 150         resetState(); 151         break; 152     case GLFW_KEY_Q: 153         app_.requestClose(); 154         break; 155     default: 156         break; 157     } 158 } 159  160 void Console::onFocus(bool focused) { 161     if (!focused) { 162         leftHeld_ = false; 163         rightHeld_ = false; 164     } 165 } 166  167 void Console::onUpdate(float dt) { 168     require(clock_.advance(dt), "Conveyor clock update failed."); 169     placeMovingObjects(); 170     const int direction = int(rightHeld_) - int(leftHeld_); 171     if (direction != 0) { 172         const graphics::AxisLine axis{app_.getCamera().target, {0, 1, 0}}; 173         require(graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(app_.getCamera(), axis, 174             float(direction) * 65.0f * dt), "Camera orbit failed."); 175     } 176     // Application::run advances Scene motions; do not update them a second time here. 177 }

   1 #pragma once   2 #include "framework/Framework.h"   3 #include <array>   4 #include <vector>   5    6 class Console {   7     graphics::Application& app_;   8     graphics::AnimationClock clock_;   9     std::array<graphics::EntityId, 5> boxes_{};  10     std::vector<graphics::EntityId> stripes_;  11     bool leftHeld_ = false;  12     bool rightHeld_ = false;  13     void placeMovingObjects();  14     void resetState();  15     void zoom(float factor);  16   17 public:  18     explicit Console(graphics::Application& app) : app_(app) {}  19     bool initScene();  20     void onKey(int key, int action, int mods);  21     void onFocus(bool focused);  22     void onUpdate(float dt);  23 };

openGL_3dAssign_21/Console.cpp
   1 #include "Console.h"   2 #include <cmath>   3 #include <iostream>   4 #include <stdexcept>   5    6 void Console::require(bool succeeded, const char* operation) const {   7     if (!succeeded) throw std::runtime_error(operation);   8 }   9   10 glm::mat4 Console::roomWorld() const {  11     const auto spin = glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), roomSpinAngle_, glm::vec3(0, 1, 0));  12     return glm::rotate(spin, roomAngle_, glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));  13 }  14   15 void Console::releaseBody(BodyObject& object) const {  16     const auto matrix = roomWorld();  17     object.body.worldPosition = object.body.position(matrix);  18     object.body.worldVelocity = glm::mat3(matrix) * object.body.localVelocity;  19     object.releasedRotation = glm::mat3(matrix);  20     object.body.released = true;  21 }  22   23 bool Console::initScene() {  24     auto& scene = app_.getScene();  25     scene.clear();  26     objects_.clear();  27     ballCount_ = 0;  28     roomAngle_ = roomSpinAngle_ = pendingRotation_ = 0;  29     floorOpen_ = dragging_ = roomSpinning_ = false;  30     const auto room = graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene, roomBounds_, true);  31     if (!room) return false;  32     room_ = *room;  33     const graphics::GlColor colors[] = {  34         {.6f, .6f, .6f}, {.35f, .5f, .8f}, {.85f, .4f, .4f},  35         {.35f, .75f, .5f}, {.75f, .5f, .8f}, {.8f, .7f, .4f}  36     };  37     for (std::size_t face = 0; face < 6; ++face) {  38         if (!scene.setFaceVertexColors(room_, face,  39                 std::vector<graphics::GlColor>(4, colors[face]))) return false;  40     }  41   42     // Different sizes share local X=0; local Z determines their initial row.  43     const glm::vec3 sizes[] = {{.65f, .65f, .65f}, {.9f, .9f, .9f}, {1.2f, 1.2f, 1.2f}};  44     const graphics::GlColor boxColors[] = {{1, .45f, .15f}, {.2f, .85f, .95f}, {.85f, .95f, .3f}};  45     for (unsigned i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {  46         BodyObject box;  47         box.body.halfExtent = sizes[i] * .5f;  48         box.body.localPosition = {0, roomBounds_.minimum.y + box.body.halfExtent.y,  49                                  -2.3f + 2.3f * i};  50         const auto entity = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(  51             scene, sizes[i], box.body.localPosition, boxColors[i]);  52         if (!entity) return false;  53         box.entity = *entity;  54         objects_.push_back(box);  55     }  56     if (!addBall()) return false;  57   58     auto& camera = app_.getCamera();  59     camera = graphics::Camera{};  60     camera.position = {0, .8f, 13};  61     camera.target = {0, .25f, 0};  62     camera.farPlane = 200;  63     camera.orthographic = false;  64     auto& render = app_.getRenderOptions();  65     render.depthTest = true;  66     render.cull = graphics::CullMode::Back;  67     render.lighting = true;  68     render.lightDirection = {.5f, 1, 2};  69     printHelp();  70     return syncScene();  71 }  72   73 bool Console::addBall() {  74     if (ballCount_ >= 5) {  75         std::cout << "공은 최대 5개입니다. R로 초기화할 수 있습니다.\n";  76         return true;  77     }  78     const float radius = .24f;  79     std::optional<glm::vec3> freePosition;  80     // A finite search checks the complete sphere extent against every live body.  81     for (int y = 0; y < 4 && !freePosition; ++y) {  82         for (int z = 0; z < 5 && !freePosition; ++z) {  83             for (int x = 0; x < 5 && !freePosition; ++x) {  84                 const glm::vec3 p(-2.2f + x * 1.1f, .1f + y * .5f, -2.6f + z * 1.3f);  85                 const graphics::Bounds3 candidate{p - glm::vec3(radius), p + glm::vec3(radius)};  86                 if (!roomBounds_.contains(p, glm::vec3(radius))) continue;  87                 bool occupied = false;  88                 for (const auto& object : objects_) {  89                     if (object.body.released) continue;  90                     const graphics::Bounds3 bodyBounds{  91                         object.body.localPosition - object.body.halfExtent,  92                         object.body.localPosition + object.body.halfExtent};  93                     if (candidate.overlaps(bodyBounds)) { occupied = true; break; }  94                 }  95                 if (!occupied) freePosition = p;  96             }  97         }  98     }  99     if (!freePosition) { 100         std::cout << "공을 생성할 빈 위치가 없습니다. 잠시 후 B를 누르세요.\n"; 101         return true; 102     } 103     auto& scene = app_.getScene(); 104     const auto sphere = scene.addSphere(radius, *freePosition); 105     if (!sphere || !scene.setTint(*sphere, {1, .85f - .12f * ballCount_, .15f + .15f * ballCount_}) 106         || !scene.setFaceCullMode(*sphere, 0, graphics::CullMode::None)) return false; 107     BodyObject ball; 108     ball.entity = *sphere; 109     ball.elastic = true; 110     ball.body.localPosition = *freePosition; 111     ball.body.halfExtent = glm::vec3(radius); 112     ball.body.localVelocity = {1.5f + .2f * ballCount_, -1.4f - .12f * ballCount_, 113                               ballCount_ % 2 ? -1.8f : 1.8f}; 114     if (floorOpen_) releaseBody(ball); 115     objects_.push_back(ball); 116     ++ballCount_; 117     std::cout << "공 생성: " << ballCount_ << "/5\n"; 118     return true; 119 } 120  121 bool Console::syncScene() { 122     auto& scene = app_.getScene(); 123     const auto matrix = roomWorld(); 124     graphics::Transform3D roomTransform; 125     roomTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix); 126     // addRoom's geometry is centered; its center must rotate about the same origin as physics. 127     roomTransform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix * glm::vec4( 128         (roomBounds_.minimum + roomBounds_.maximum) * .5f, 1)); 129     if (!scene.setTransform(room_, roomTransform) 130         || !scene.setFaceVisible(room_, 5, !floorOpen_)) return false; 131     for (const auto& object : objects_) { 132         graphics::Transform3D transform; 133         transform.basis = object.body.position(matrix); 134         if (!object.elastic) 135             transform.rotation = object.body.released ? object.releasedRotation : glm::mat3(matrix); 136         if (!scene.setTransform(object.entity, transform)) return false; 137     } 138     return true; 139 } 140  141 void Console::onKey(int key, int action, int mods) { 142     if (!focused_ || (action != GLFW_PRESS && action != GLFW_REPEAT)) return; 143     const bool uppercase = ((mods & GLFW_MOD_SHIFT) != 0) != ((mods & GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK) != 0); 144     if (key == GLFW_KEY_Z) { 145         require(graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(app_.getCamera(), 146             {0, 0, uppercase ? -.35f : .35f}), "카메라 Z 이동 실패"); 147     } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_Y) { 148         require(graphics::PatternSystem::yawCamera(app_.getCamera(), uppercase ? 4.0f : -4.0f), 149                 "카메라 로컬 Y 자전 실패"); 150     } else if (action == GLFW_PRESS) { 151         if (key == GLFW_KEY_B) { 152             require(addBall() && syncScene(), "공 추가 또는 표시변환 실패"); 153         } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_E || key == GLFW_KEY_F) { 154             floorOpen_ = !floorOpen_; 155             if (floorOpen_) { 156                 for (auto& object : objects_) 157                     if (!object.body.released) releaseBody(object); 158             } 159             require(syncScene(), "바닥 가시성 변경 실패"); 160             std::cout << (floorOpen_ ? "바닥 열림: 모든 내부 공과 상자가 즉시 중력 낙하\n" 161                                      : "바닥 닫힘: 이미 이탈한 객체는 계속 낙하\n"); 162         } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_V) { 163             roomSpinning_ = !roomSpinning_; 164             std::cout << (roomSpinning_ ? "방 Y축 자전 시작\n" : "방 Y축 자전 정지\n"); 165         } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_R) { 166             require(initScene(), "장면 초기화 실패"); 167         } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_Q || key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) { 168             app_.requestClose(); 169         } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H) { 170             printHelp(); 171         } 172     } 173 } 174  175 void Console::onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event) { 176     if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) return; 177     if (event.action == GLFW_RELEASE) { dragging_ = false; return; } 178     if (event.action == GLFW_PRESS && focused_) { 179         dragging_ = true; 180         previousMouseX_ = event.windowX; 181     } 182 } 183  184 void Console::onCursor(double x, double) { 185     if (!dragging_ || !focused_) return; 186     const float delta = glm::radians(float((previousMouseX_ - x) * .3)); 187     previousMouseX_ = x; 188     if (!std::isfinite(delta)) return; 189     // Positive Z rotation is counterclockwise when looking through the front opening. 190     roomAngle_ = std::remainder(roomAngle_ + delta, glm::radians(360.0f)); 191     pendingRotation_ += delta; 192 } 193  194 void Console::onFocus(bool focused) { 195     focused_ = focused; 196     dragging_ = false; 197     pendingRotation_ = 0; 198 } 199  200 void Console::onUpdate(float dt) { 201     if (!focused_) return; 202     const float spinSpeed = roomSpinning_ ? glm::radians(30.0f) : 0; 203     roomSpinAngle_ = std::remainder(roomSpinAngle_ + spinSpeed * dt, glm::radians(360.0f)); 204     const auto matrix = roomWorld(); 205     const auto spin = glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), roomSpinAngle_, glm::vec3(0, 1, 0)); 206     const glm::vec3 angularVelocity = glm::vec3(0, spinSpeed, 0) 207         + glm::mat3(spin) * glm::vec3(0, 0, dt > 0 ? pendingRotation_ / dt : 0); 208     if (dt > 0) pendingRotation_ = 0; 209     for (auto& object : objects_) { 210         const bool wasReleased = object.body.released; 211         require(graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(object.body, roomBounds_, matrix, 212             dt, floorOpen_, object.elastic, {0, -9.8f, 0}, angularVelocity), 213             "실내 반사/미끄럼/낙하 계산 실패"); 214         if (!wasReleased && object.body.released) object.releasedRotation = glm::mat3(matrix); 215     } 216     require(syncScene(), "장면 표시변환 실패"); 217 } 218  219 void Console::printHelp() const { 220     std::cout << "실습 21: 실내 공 반사, 기울어진 바닥의 상자, 바닥 개방\n" 221               << "z/Z: 카메라 월드 Z +/-, y/Y: 화면 로컬 Y 시계/반시계 자전\n" 222               << "Shift XOR Caps Lock으로 소문자/대문자 구분. 카메라 키는 반복 허용.\n" 223               << "B: 공 추가(초기 1개, 최대 5개), e/F: 바닥 열기/닫기\n" 224               << "바닥 개방 즉시 모든 공/상자 중력 낙하. 개방 중 새 공도 낙하.\n" 225               << "v: 방 Y축 30도/초 자전 시작/정지, 내부 벽 Front 컬링 유지\n" 226               << "왼쪽 버튼 좌우 드래그: 공간 Z축 회전, 기울기에 따라 상자 미끄럼\n" 227               << "R: 전체 초기화, H: 도움말, Q 또는 Esc: 종료\n" 228               << "B/e/F/v/R/H/Q는 누른 순간 한 번만 적용. 포커스 상실 시 드래그 해제.\n"; 229 }
openGL_3dAssign_21/Console.h
   1 #pragma once   2 #include "framework/Framework.h"   3    4 class Console {   5     struct BodyObject {   6         graphics::EntityId entity = 0;   7         graphics::RoomBody body;   8         bool elastic = false;   9         glm::mat3 releasedRotation{1};  10     };  11     graphics::Application& app_;  12     const graphics::Bounds3 roomBounds_{{-3, -2, -3.5f}, {3, 2.5f, 3.5f}};  13     graphics::EntityId room_ = 0;  14     std::vector<BodyObject> objects_;  15     unsigned ballCount_ = 0;  16     float roomAngle_ = 0;  17     float roomSpinAngle_ = 0;  18     float pendingRotation_ = 0;  19     bool roomSpinning_ = false;  20     bool floorOpen_ = false;  21     bool dragging_ = false;  22     bool focused_ = true;  23     double previousMouseX_ = 0;  24   25     glm::mat4 roomWorld() const;  26     bool addBall();  27     void releaseBody(BodyObject& object) const;  28     bool syncScene();  29     void require(bool succeeded, const char* operation) const;  30     void printHelp() const;  31   32 public:  33     explicit Console(graphics::Application& app) : app_(app) {}  34     bool initScene();  35     void onKey(int key, int action, int mods);  36     void onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event);  37     void onCursor(double x, double y);  38     void onFocus(bool focused);  39     void onUpdate(float dt);  40 };
openGL_3dAssign_21/main.cpp
   1 #include "Console.h"   2 #include <exception>   3 #include <iostream>   4    5 int main() {   6     try {   7         graphics::Application app;   8         Console sample(app);   9         app.setInitHandler([&](graphics::Application&) { return sample.initScene(); });  10         app.setModifiedKeyHandler([&](graphics::Application&, int key, int action, int mods) {  11             sample.onKey(key, action, mods);  12         });  13         app.setMouseHandler([&](graphics::Application&, const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {  14             sample.onMouse(event);  15         });  16         app.setCursorHandler([&](graphics::Application&, double x, double y) { sample.onCursor(x, y); });  17         app.setFocusHandler([&](graphics::Application&, bool focused) { sample.onFocus(focused); });  18         app.setUpdateHandler([&](graphics::Application&, float dt) { sample.onUpdate(dt); });  19         graphics::ApplicationConfig config;  20         config.title = "Practice 21 - Bouncing balls and rotating room";  21         config.width = 1100;  22         config.height = 800;  23         config.diagnostics = {true, false};  24         return app.run(config);  25     } catch (const std::exception& error) {  26         std::cerr << "실습 21 종료 원인: " << error.what() << '\n';  27     } catch (...) {  28         std::cerr << "실습 21 종료 원인: 표준 예외가 아닌 예외. 앞선 프레임워크 진단을 확인하세요.\n";  29     }  30     return 1;  31 }

   1 #include "Console.h"   2 #include <algorithm>   3 #include <cmath>   4 #include <iostream>   5 #include <stdexcept>   6    7 namespace {   8 graphics::EntityId requireEntity(std::optional<graphics::EntityId> id,const char* reason) {   9     if(!id)throw std::runtime_error(reason);  10     return *id;  11 }  12 }  13 graphics::EntityId Console::box(glm::vec3 size,glm::vec3 position,graphics::EntityId parent) {  14     const auto id=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(  15         app_.getScene(),size,position,{1,1,1},parent),"탱크 상자 생성 실패: 크기·위치·부모 ID를 확인하세요.");  16     colored_.push_back(id);  17     return id;  18 }  19 bool Console::initScene() {  20     reset();  21     std::cout << "실습 22: 같은 장면을 원근 / XZ 평면도 / XY 정면도로 표시\n"  22               << "방향키 이동; d 하부 높이; t 중앙 자전; l 상부 교환\n"  23               << "g 포신 반대 회전; p 깃대 반대 기울기; x/X,z/Z 카메라 이동\n"  24               << "y/Y 카메라 시선 회전; r/R 공전(재입력 정지); O/o 색 변경; c 초기화; q 종료\n";  25     return true;  26 }  27 void Console::reset() {  28     auto& scene=app_.getScene();  29     scene.clear();  30     colored_.clear();  31     pressed_.fill(false);  32     position_=glm::vec3(0);  33     lowerTime_=centralAngle_=gunAngle_=poleTime_=swapProgress_=0;  34     swapDirection_=1;  35     cameraX_=cameraZ_=cameraYaw_=cameraOrbit_=0;  36     lowerRunning_=centralRunning_=gunRunning_=poleRunning_=swapRunning_=swapStarted_=false;  37     colorVersion_=0;  38     root_=requireEntity(scene.addGroup(),"탱크 루트 생성 실패.");  39     central_=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,root_,{0,.6f,0}),  40         "중앙 관절 생성 실패.");  41     const auto ground=requireEntity(scene.addGroup(),"바닥 그룹 생성 실패.");  42     box({12,.1f,12},{0,-.05f,0},ground);  43     for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i)lower_[i]=box({.65f,.6f,1.8f},{i==0?-.8f:.8f,.3f,0},root_);  44     box({2.3f,.25f,1.4f},{0,.125f,0},central_);  45     for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {  46         const float sign=i==0?-1.0f:1.0f;  47         upper_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,central_,{sign*.65f,.25f,0}),  48             "상부 관절 생성 실패.");  49         guns_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,upper_[i],{0,.3f,0}),  50             "포신 중심 피벗 생성 실패.");  51         poles_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,upper_[i],{0,.6f,0}),  52             "깃대 밑점 피벗 생성 실패.");  53         box({.65f,.6f,.8f},{0,.3f,0},upper_[i]);  54         // Gun offsets stay relative to the original body-center pivot (0,.3,0).  55         box({.14f,.14f,1.1f},{0,-.05f,.95f},guns_[i]);  56         box({.08f,.85f,.08f},{0,.425f,0},poles_[i]);  57     }  58     auto& camera=app_.getCamera();  59     camera=graphics::Camera{};  60     camera.position={-5,4.5f,7};  61     camera.target={0,.7f,0};  62     camera.orthographic=false;  63     camera.orthographicHalfExtent=6.5f;  64     app_.getRenderOptions()=graphics::RenderOptions{};  65     app_.getRenderOptions().lighting=true;  66     recolor();  67     apply();  68 }  69 void Console::recolor() {  70     const std::array<graphics::GlColor,10> palette{  71         graphics::GlColor{.3f,.35f,.4f},{.25f,.6f,.3f},{.55f,.35f,.2f},{.2f,.55f,.75f},  72         {.7f,.75f,.2f},{.9f,.4f,.15f},{1,.8f,.2f},{.4f,.7f,.8f},{.7f,.25f,.6f},{.6f,.9f,.4f}};  73     for(std::size_t i=0;i<colored_.size();++i)  74         if(!app_.getScene().setTint(colored_[i],palette[(i+colorVersion_)%palette.size()]))  75             throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 색상 적용 실패.");  76 }  77 void Console::apply() {  78     auto& scene=app_.getScene();  79     if(!scene.setPosition(root_,position_))throw std::runtime_error("탱크 위치 적용 실패.");  80     const float factor=1+.25f*std::sin(lowerTime_),height=.6f*factor;  81     if(!scene.setPosition(central_,{0,height,0})  82         ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,central_,{0,1,0},centralAngle_))  83         throw std::runtime_error("중앙 관절 변환 적용 실패.");  84     for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {  85         const float sign=i==0?-1.0f:1.0f;  86         graphics::Transform3D low;  87         low.basis={sign*.8f,height*.5f,0};  88         low.scale={1,factor,1};  89         const float a=3.14159265f*swapProgress_;  90         const glm::vec3 upperPosition{sign*.65f*std::cos(a),.25f,sign*.65f*std::sin(a)};  91         if(!scene.setTransform(lower_[i],low)||!scene.setPosition(upper_[i],upperPosition)  92             ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,guns_[i],{0,1,0},sign*gunAngle_)  93             ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,poles_[i],{1,0,0},sign*60*std::sin(poleTime_)))  94             throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 변환 적용 실패.");  95     }  96 }  97 void Console::onKey(int key,int action,int mods) {  98     const bool upper=((mods&GLFW_MOD_SHIFT)!=0)!=((mods&GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK)!=0);  99     if(key==GLFW_KEY_X||key==GLFW_KEY_Z) { 100         if(action==GLFW_PRESS||action==GLFW_RELEASE) { 101             float& direction=key==GLFW_KEY_X?cameraX_:cameraZ_; 102             direction=action==GLFW_RELEASE?0:(upper?-1.0f:1.0f); 103         } 104         return; 105     } 106     if(key>=0&&key<=GLFW_KEY_LAST) { 107         if(action==GLFW_PRESS)pressed_[key]=true; 108         else if(action==GLFW_RELEASE)pressed_[key]=false; 109     } 110     if(action!=GLFW_PRESS)return; 111     const float direction=upper?1.0f:-1.0f; 112     switch(key) { 113     case GLFW_KEY_D: 114         lowerRunning_=!lowerRunning_; 115         break; 116     case GLFW_KEY_T: 117         centralRunning_=!centralRunning_; 118         break; 119     case GLFW_KEY_G: 120         gunRunning_=!gunRunning_; 121         break; 122     case GLFW_KEY_P: 123         poleRunning_=!poleRunning_; 124         break; 125     case GLFW_KEY_L: 126         if(!swapStarted_){ 127             swapStarted_=true; 128             swapRunning_=true; 129         } 130         else if(!swapRunning_&&(swapProgress_==0||swapProgress_==1)){ 131             swapDirection_=-swapDirection_; 132             swapRunning_=true; 133         } 134         else swapRunning_=!swapRunning_; 135         break; 136     case GLFW_KEY_Y: 137         cameraYaw_=cameraYaw_==direction?0:direction; 138         break; 139     case GLFW_KEY_R: 140         cameraOrbit_=cameraOrbit_==direction?0:direction; 141         break; 142     case GLFW_KEY_O: 143         colorVersion_=(colorVersion_+1)%10; 144         recolor(); 145         break; 146     case GLFW_KEY_C: 147         reset(); 148         break; 149     case GLFW_KEY_Q: 150     case GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE: 151         app_.requestClose(); 152         break; 153     default: 154         break; 155     } 156 } 157 void Console::onUpdate(float dt) { 158     glm::vec3 movement{float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_RIGHT])-float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_LEFT]),0, 159         float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_DOWN])-float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_UP])}; 160     if(glm::length(movement)>0)position_+=glm::normalize(movement)*dt*1.3f; 161     // 12x12 floor minus a 2.5-unit radius covers every animated part's sweep. 162     position_.x=std::clamp(position_.x,-3.5f,3.5f); 163     position_.z=std::clamp(position_.z,-3.5f,3.5f); 164     if(lowerRunning_)lowerTime_=std::fmod(lowerTime_+dt,6.283185307f); 165     if(centralRunning_)centralAngle_=std::fmod(centralAngle_+45*dt,360.0f); 166     if(gunRunning_)gunAngle_= std::fmod(gunAngle_+60*dt,-360.0f); 167     if(poleRunning_)poleTime_=std::fmod(poleTime_+dt,6.283185307f); 168     if(swapRunning_) { 169         swapProgress_=std::clamp(swapProgress_+swapDirection_*dt*.5f,0.0f,1.0f); 170         if(swapProgress_==0||swapProgress_==1)swapRunning_=false; 171     } 172     auto camera=app_.getCamera(); 173     const glm::vec3 delta{cameraX_*2*dt,0,cameraZ_*2*dt}; 174     if(!graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera,delta) 175         ||(cameraYaw_!=0&&!graphics::PatternSystem::yawCamera(camera,cameraYaw_*35*dt)) 176         ||(cameraOrbit_!=0&&!graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera,{{0,0,0},{0,1,0}},cameraOrbit_*25*dt))) 177         throw std::runtime_error("카메라 갱신 실패: 위치·시선·회전축을 확인하세요."); 178     app_.getCamera()=camera; 179     apply(); 180 } 181 void Console::onFocus(bool focused) { 182     if(!focused){ 183         pressed_.fill(false); 184         cameraX_=cameraZ_=0; 185     } 186 } 187  188 bool Console::onRender() { 189     const auto size=app_.getFramebufferSize(); 190     if(size[0]<3||size[1]<2)return true; 191     const auto passes=graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews( 192         app_.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],app_.getRenderOptions()); 193     if(passes.size()!=3)throw std::runtime_error("세 카메라 뷰 생성 실패: 프레임버퍼 크기와 카메라를 확인하세요."); 194     return app_.renderViews(passes); 195 }

   1 #include "Console.h"   2 #include <algorithm>   3 #include <stdexcept>   4 #include <iostream>   5 namespace {   6 void require(bool ok,const char* message) {if(!ok) throw std::runtime_error(message);}   7 }   8 bool Console::initScene() {   9     auto& scene=app_.getScene();scene.clear();obstacles_.clear();  10     body_=graphics::CharacterBody{};body_.feet={-3,0,-3};body_.speed=1;  11     body_.height=1.8f;body_.halfWidth={.5f,.5f};  12     doors_.fill(0);open_.fill(false);activeWall_.reset();walking_=true;  13     const auto stage=graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene,bounds_,false);  14     if(!stage) return false;stage_=*stage;  15     for(std::size_t face=0;face<4;++face) {  16         if(!graphics::PatternSystem::openRoomWall(scene,stage_,face,0)  17             || !scene.setOutline(stage_,face,graphics::OutlineMode::FeatureEdges)) return false;  21     std::cout << "실습 23 난수 시드=" << randomSeed_ << "; 초기화 시 기존 난수 엔진의 다음 수열을 사용합니다.\n";  22     std::vector<graphics::Bounds3> occupied{{{-3.8f,0,-3.8f},{-2.2f,2,-2.2f}}};  23     for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i) {  24         const glm::vec3 size{.9f+.2f*i,.6f+.25f*i,.9f+.15f*i};  37     const bool upper=((mods&GLFW_MOD_SHIFT)!=0)!=((mods&GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK)!=0);  38     auto& camera=app_.getCamera();  39     if(key==GLFW_KEY_X || key==GLFW_KEY_Z) {  40         glm::vec3 delta(0);delta[key==GLFW_KEY_X?0:2]=upper?-.35f:.35f;  41         require(graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera,delta),"실습 23 카메라 이동 실패.");return;  42     }  43     if(key==GLFW_KEY_Y) {require(graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera,{camera.target,{0,1,0}},upper?4.0f:-4.0f),"실습 23 카메라 공전 실패.");return;}  44     if(key==GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD) body_.speed=std::min(3.0f,body_.speed+.1f);  45     if(key==GLFW_KEY_MINUS || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) body_.speed=std::max(0.0f,body_.speed-.1f);  46     if(key==GLFW_KEY_W) {body_.direction={0,0,1};walking_=true;}  47     if(key==GLFW_KEY_S) {body_.direction={0,0,-1};walking_=true;}  48     if(key==GLFW_KEY_A) {body_.direction={-1,0,0};walking_=true;}  49     if(key==GLFW_KEY_D) {body_.direction={1,0,0};walking_=true;}  50     if(action!=GLFW_PRESS) return;  51     if(key==GLFW_KEY_O) {  52         if(activeWall_) open_[*activeWall_]=!open_[*activeWall_];  53         else {  54             activeWall_=graphics::PatternSystem::cameraFacingWall(app_.getScene(),stage_,camera);  55             if(activeWall_) open_[*activeWall_]=true;  56         }  57         if(activeWall_) std::cout<<"선택한 벽="<<*activeWall_<<(open_[*activeWall_]?" 열기\n":" 원상복귀\n");  58     }  59     if(key==GLFW_KEY_SPACE) walking_=!walking_;  60     if(key==GLFW_KEY_J) graphics::CollisionSystem::jump(body_,5.5f);  61     if(key==GLFW_KEY_I) require(initScene(),"실습 23 초기화 실패: 앞선 API 오류를 확인하세요.");  62     if(key==GLFW_KEY_Q || key==GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) app_.requestClose();  63 }  64 void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {  65     const float requested=body_.speed;  66     if(!walking_) body_.speed=0;  67     const auto result=graphics::CollisionSystem::stepCharacter(body_,bounds_,obstacles_,dt);  68     body_.speed=requested;  69     require(result.has_value(),"실습 23 캐릭터 충돌 계산 실패.");  70     const float yaw=glm::degrees(std::atan2(body_.direction.x,body_.direction.z));  71     require(graphics::PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig(app_.getScene(),rig_,body_.feet,yaw,body_.walkDistance,body_.speed),"실습 23 로봇 관절 자세 적용 실패.");  72     for(std::size_t face=0;face<doors_.size();++face) {  73         doors_[face]=std::clamp(doors_[face]+(open_[face]?1.0f:-1.0f)*dt,0.0f,1.0f);  74         require(graphics::PatternSystem::openRoomWall(app_.getScene(),stage_,face,doors_[face]),"실습 23 벽 개방·복원 실패.");  75     }  76     if(activeWall_ && !open_[*activeWall_] && doors_[*activeWall_]==0) activeWall_.reset();  77 }

   1 #include "Console.h"   2 #include <iostream>   3    4 bool Console::choosePath(int kind) {   5     graphics::PathMotion path;   6     path.durationSeconds = 6;   7     const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);   8    9     if (kind == 1) {  10         path.path = graphics::PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});  11     } else if (kind == 2) {  12         path.path = graphics::PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});  13     } else if (kind == 3) {  14         path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polygon({  15             {1, -.5f, 0},  16             {2.4f, -.4f, 0},  17             {2.1f, .6f, 0},  18             {1.4f, .8f, 0},  19             {.9f, .2f, 0}  20         });  21     } else if (kind == 4) {  22         path.path = [center](double u) {  23             const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;  24             return center + glm::vec3(  25                 float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)),  26                 float(.6 * std::sin(angle)),  27                 float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));  28         };  29         path.interpolation = graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep;  30     } else if (kind == 5) {  31         path.path = graphics::PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});  32     } else if (kind == 6) {  33         path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polyline({  34             {1, -.5f, 0},  35             {2.4f, -.5f, 0},  36             {2, .6f, 0}  37         });  38         path.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;  39     } else {  40         return false;  41     }  42   43     graphics::Motion motion;  44     auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();  45     if (!pathId_ || !motion.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) {  46         return false;  47     }  48     if (!motions.setMotion(*pathId_, motion) || !motions.seekMotion(*pathId_, 0)) {  49         return false;  50     }  51     std::cout << "경로 " << kind << ": 시작 시각 0초로 변경\n";  52     return true;  53 }  54   55 bool Console::initScene() {  56     demo_=Demo::Motion;  57     auto& scene = app_.getScene();  58     scene.clear();  59     paused_ = false;  60     spinEnabled_ = true;  61     globalEnabled_ = false;  62     reflection_ = 1;  63     insertedLayer_.reset();  64   65     const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});  66     const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});  67     if (!first || !second) {  68         return false;  69     }  70     if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f})  71         || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) {  72         return false;  73     }  74   75     auto& motions = scene.getMotions();  76     pathId_ = motions.createMotion(graphics::Motion{});  77     graphics::RotationMotion spin;  78     spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;  79     spinId_ = motions.createSpin(spin);  80     graphics::RotationMotion global;  81     global.degreesPerSecond = 15;  82     globalId_ = motions.createOrbit(global);  83     if (!pathId_ || !spinId_ || !globalId_ || !choosePath(1)) {  84         return false;  85     }  86     if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, false)  87         || !motions.bindMotion(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId_)) {  88         return false;  89     }  90   91     const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer());  92     const auto reflection = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});  93     if (!normalPath || !reflection) {  94         return false;  95     }  96     graphics::LayerConfig mirror;  97     mirror.transform = *reflection;  98     mirrorLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);  99     if (!mirrorLayer_) { 100         return false; 101     } 102     const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer_); 103     normalSpinLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath); 104     if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer_) { 105         return false; 106     } 107     const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath); 108     if (!mirroredSpin 109         || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId_) 110         || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId_) 111         || !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer_, *spinId_) 112         || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId_) 113         || !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer_) 114         || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) { 115         return false; 116     } 117     if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first) 118         || !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second) 119         || !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) { 120         return false; 121     } 122  123     auto& camera = app_.getCamera(); 124     camera.position = {5, 3, 8}; 125     camera.target = {0, 0, 0}; 126     camera.up = {0, 1, 0}; 127     printHelp(); 128     return true; 129 } 130  131 bool Console::changeReflection() { 132     const int next = (reflection_ + 1) % 4; 133     std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1); 134     if (next == 1) { 135         matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0}); 136     } else if (next == 2) { 137         matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0}); 138     } else if (next == 3) { 139         matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(graphics::AxisLine{}); 140     } 141     if (!mirrorLayer_ || !matrix) { 142         return false; 143     } 144     graphics::LayerConfig config; 145     config.transform = *matrix; 146     if (!app_.getScene().getMotions().setLayer(*mirrorLayer_, config)) { 147         return false; 148     } 149     reflection_ = next; 150     const char* names[] = {"없음", "YZ평면 반사", "원점 점대칭", "Y축 직선대칭"}; 151     std::cout << "반사: " << names[reflection_] << '\n'; 152     return true; 153 } 154  155 bool Console::toggleInsertedLayer() { 156     auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions(); 157     if (insertedLayer_) { 158         if (!motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer_)) { 159             return false; 160         } 161         insertedLayer_.reset(); 162         std::cout << "추가 크기 레이어 삭제\n"; 163         return true; 164     } 165     const auto scale = graphics::TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f)); 166     if (!normalSpinLayer_ || !scale) { 167         return false; 168     } 169     graphics::LayerConfig config; 170     config.space = graphics::TransformSpace::Local; 171     config.transform = *scale; 172     insertedLayer_ = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer_, config); 173     if (!insertedLayer_) { 174         return false; 175     } 176     std::cout << "첫 객체에 1.5배 Local 크기 레이어 삽입\n"; 177     return true; 178 } 179  180 void Console::onKey(int key, int action) { 181     if (action != GLFW_PRESS) { 182         return; 183     } 184     if(key==GLFW_KEY_7 || key==GLFW_KEY_8 || key==GLFW_KEY_0) { 185         const bool ok=key==GLFW_KEY_7?initGrid():key==GLFW_KEY_8?initRoom():initScene(); 186         if(!ok) app_.requestClose();return; 187     } 188     if(key==GLFW_KEY_V) {threeViews_=!threeViews_;return;} 189     if(key==GLFW_KEY_D) {trace_=!trace_;graphics::Diagnostics::configure({true,trace_});return;} 190     if(key==GLFW_KEY_F && demo_==Demo::Room) {floorOpen_=!floorOpen_;return;} 191     if(demo_!=Demo::Motion) { 192         if(key==GLFW_KEY_SPACE) clock_.playing=!clock_.playing; 193         else if(key==GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) app_.requestClose(); 194         else if(key==GLFW_KEY_H) printHelp(); 195         return; 196     } 197     auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions(); 198     bool ok = true; 199     if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) { 200         app_.requestClose(); 201     } else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) { 202         ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1); 203     } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) { 204         const bool playing = paused_; 209         if (ok) { 210             paused_ = !paused_; 211             std::cout << (paused_ ? 267 } 268  269 bool Console::initGrid() { 270     auto& scene=app_.getScene();scene.clear();demo_=Demo::Grid;clock_.reset();gridBoxes_.clear(); 271     graphics::GridLayout grid;grid.origin={-3,-.1f,-2};grid.extent={6,.2f,4};grid.cells={6,1,4}; 292     return true; 293 } 294 void Console::onUpdate(float dt) { 295     if(demo_==Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing) return; 296     if(!clock_.advance(dt)) {app_.requestClose();return;} 297     auto& scene=app_.getScene();bool ok=true; 298     if(demo_==Demo::Grid) { 299         for(std::size_t i=0;i<gridBoxes_.size();++i) 300             ok=graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene,gridBoxes_[i],distancePath_,clock_.seconds+distancePath_.length()*i/5)&&ok; 301     } else { 302         const float angle=.4f*float(std::sin(clock_.seconds)); 303         const auto matrix=glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),angle,glm::vec3(0,0,1)); 304         graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;roomTransform.rotation=glm::mat3(matrix);roomTransform.basis=glm::vec3(matrix*glm::vec4(0,.5f,0,1)); 305         ok=scene.setTransform(room_,roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_,5,!floorOpen_) 306             && graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_,{{-2,-1,-2},{2,2,2}},matrix,dt,floorOpen_,false,{0,-9.8f,0},{0,0,.4f*float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))}); 307         graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;cubeTransform.basis=roomBody_.position(matrix); 308         if(!roomBody_.released) cubeTransform.rotation=glm::mat3(matrix); 309         ok=scene.setTransform(roomCube_,cubeTransform)&&ok; 310     } 311     if(!ok) app_.requestClose(); 312 } 313 bool Console::onRender() { 314     if(!threeViews_) return app_.renderScene(); 315     const auto size=app_.getFramebufferSize();if(size[0]<3 || size[1]<2) return true; 316     return app_.renderViews(graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],app_.getRenderOptions())); 317 }
standard/Console.h
   1 #pragma once   2 #include "framework/Framework.h"   3    4 class Console {   5     graphics::Application& app_;   6     std::optional<graphics::MotionId> pathId_;   7     std::optional<graphics::MotionId> spinId_;   8     std::optional<graphics::MotionId> globalId_;   9     std::optional<graphics::LayerId> mirrorLayer_;  10     std::optional<graphics::LayerId> normalSpinLayer_;  11     std::optional<graphics::LayerId> insertedLayer_;  12     bool paused_ = false;  13     bool spinEnabled_ = true;  14     bool globalEnabled_ = false;  15     int reflection_ = 1;  16     enum class Demo { Motion, Grid, Room };  17     Demo demo_=Demo::Motion;  18     graphics::AnimationClock clock_;  19     graphics::DistancePath distancePath_;  20     std::vector<graphics::EntityId> gridBoxes_;  21     graphics::RoomBody roomBody_;  22     graphics::EntityId room_=0, roomCube_=0;  23     bool threeViews_=false, trace_=false, floorOpen_=false;  24     bool initGrid();  25     bool initRoom();  26   27     bool choosePath(int kind);  28     bool changeReflection();  29     bool toggleInsertedLayer();  30     void printHelp() const;  31   32 public:  33     explicit Console(graphics::Application& app) : app_(app) {}  34     bool initScene();  35     void onKey(int key, int action);  36     void onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event);  37     void onUpdate(float dt);  38     bool onRender();  39 };
standard/main.cpp
   1 #include "Console.h"   2    3 int main() {   4     graphics::Application app;   5     Console sample(app);   6    7     app.setInitHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {   8         return sample.initScene();   9     });  10     app.setKeyHandler([&](graphics::Application&, int key, int action) {  11         sample.onKey(key, action);  12     });  13     app.setMouseHandler([&](graphics::Application&, const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {  14         sample.onMouse(event);  15     });  16     app.setUpdateHandler([&](graphics::Application&,float dt) {sample.onUpdate(dt);});  17     app.setRenderHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {return sample.onRender();});  18   19     graphics::ApplicationConfig config;  20     config.title = "standard - Motion, Grid, diagnostics and render passes";  21     return app.run(config);  22 }
소스와 graphify AST/정의 앵커를 대조한 학습 자료다. 실행 순서 설명은 실제 콜백 코드로 보완했다. 그래프의 동명 심볼·함수 포인터 간선은 확정 호출로 해석하지 않는다. 빌드·실행·테스트·검증기·브라우저 시각 확인은 수행하지 않았고 사용자에게 맡긴다. ZIP을 만들지 않았다.
